iṉṉum pāyirattuḷ "muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa veṇṇi" eṉṟu pāyiram ceytār kūṟutalāṉē uṇarkaveṉa iyaikka. eppaṭi uṇartaleṉiṉ? ivvāciriyar kūṟiya ataṉaiyē īṇṭum (pāyirattuḷ) "muntunūlkaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa veṇṇi" eṉap pāyirakārar kūṟiṉāreṉa uṇartal. muntu nūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa veṇṇit tokuttāṉ eṉa vaḻinūl ilakkaṇamum peṟappaṭa pāyirakārar kūṟiyataṟkuviti ceyyuḷiyalilum marapiyalilum tolkāppiyar kūṟiya vitiyē yeṉpatām. 
innūlāciriyar vaḻinūlceytaṟku vitiyāṇṭup peṟṟā reṉiṉ mutalvaṉ kūṟiṟṟilaṉēṉum avaṉ nūl ceyta muṟaiyē tamakkilakkaṇamāmeṉṟu karutic ceytāreṉka. mutalvaṉ ceyta muṟaiyē tamakkum ilakkaṇamākak koṇṭu nūl ceytāreṉi ṉum oruvaṉ ceytatai mīḷavuñ ceytalāṟ payaṉiṉṟē yeṉṉu mākkēpattai nīkkum poruṭṭē muṟaippaṭa eṇṇi yeṉṟā reṉka.
pāyiram muṟṟiṟṟu. 
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	aṅṅaṉam `mayaṅkivaruvaṉa evaiyeṉiṉ? eṉpatu mutal.... eṉka,' eṉpatu varaiyum uḷḷa vākkiyam māṇākkaṉ viḷaṅkum poruṭṭup pērāciriyar karuttait taḻuvi yāmeḻutiya vākkiyamākum. 


eḻuttatikāram
eḻuttiṉatu atikārattaiyuṭaiyatu eḻuttatikāram eṉak kāraṇappeyarāyiṟṟu. atikāram-muṟaimai. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇattai muṟaimaippaṭa viritturaikkum paṭalam eḻuttatikāram eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. eḻuttāvatu makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭum moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamākiya oliyākum. tolkāppiyaṉārāl eḻutteṉac collappaṭṭavai akara mutal ṉakara mīṟākavuḷḷa muppatum kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukām, āytam eṉṉum mūṉṟum āka muppattu mūṉṟākum. ivaṟṟiṟku eḻutteṉṉum peyar tamakku neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉṉē tōṉṟi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṉai eḻutteṉappaṭupa, akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa eṉavarum cūttirattāl tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
vilaṅku mutaliya aḵṟiṇaiyuyirkaḷiṉṟum makkaḷai vēṟu pirittu uyartiṇai māntarāka uyarttum aṟitaṟ karuviyāka viḷaṅkuvatu moḻi. attakaiya moḻikaḷuḷ pēccu vaḻakkoṉṟē peṟṟu eḻutturup peṟātaṉavum uḷḷaṉa. pēccu moḻi ōriṭattum orukālattumē payaṉpaṭum. eḻuttumoḻiyō taṉmai, muṉṉilai, paṭarkkaiyākiya mūviṭattum iṟappu, nikaḻvu, etirvu ākiya mukkālattum oppap payaṉ taruvatākum. paṉaiyōlaikaḷilum kallilum piṟa poruḷkaḷilum eṇṇaṅkaḷai eḻuttāṟ poṟittuvaikkum vaḻakkam pallāyira āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē nam tamiḻ makkaḷāl kaikkoḷḷappeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. cellun tēettup peyarmaruṅ kaṟimār, kallēṟin teḻutiya nallarai marāatta, kaṭavuḷōṅkiya kāṭēcu kavalai (malaipaṭu - 394-395) eṉavum, peyarum piṭum eḻuti yatartoṟum, pīlicūṭṭiya piṟaṅkunilai naṭukal (akam - 131) eṉavum peṟikaṇṭaḻikkum āvaṇamākkaḷiṉ (akam - 77) eṉavum varum caṅka ilakkiyat toṭarkaḷāl icceyti pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
makkaḷ tammāṟ pēcappaṭum moḻiyilamainta olikaḷait taṉittaṉiyākap pirittaṟiyum uṇarvupeṟṟa piṉṉarttāṉ karutturuvākiya avvolikaḷaik kaṭpulaṉāka varivaṭivil eḻututalkūṭum. aṟiñarkaḷatu naṉmuyaṟciyāl olikaḷukkuriya varivaṭivaṅka ḷamainta piṉputāṉ avvolikaḷukku eḻut teṉṉuṅ kāraṇappeyar vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. iv vuṇmai eḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻuttē1 eṉavarum paḻaiya cūttirat toṭarāl aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭamai kāṇka.
meyyeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟutalum ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ puḷḷipeṟutalum kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟutalum makarakkuṟukkam meykkuriya mēṟpuḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyoru puḷḷipeṟutalum ākiya eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟait tolkāppiyaṉār tam nūlakattu viḷakkiyuḷḷār. ākavē tolkāppiyaṉārkku neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻ muṉṉōr akara mutal ṉakara viṟuvāyuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷukkuriya varivaṭivaṅkaḷai yamaittut tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉai oḻuṅku ceytuḷḷamai naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
- veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 162-164

nūṉmarapu
ivvatikārattāṟ collappaṭum eḻuttilakkaṇattiṉai ōrāṟṟāṟ ṟokuttuṇarttutaliṉ nūṉmarapeṉṉum peyarttu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, ittolkāppiyameṉum nūṟku marapān tuṇaikku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟait tokut tuṇarttutaliṉ nūṉ marapeṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, aḵtāvatu nūliṉatu marapu paṟṟiya peyar kūṟutal..................... malaikaṭalyāṟu eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu ulakamarapu paṟṟiya peyar pōlātu īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭum eḻuttu, kuṟil, neṭil, uyir, mey eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup peyarkaḷ nūliṉkaṇ āḷutaṟporuṭṭu mutaṉūlāciriyaṉāṟ ceytukoḷḷap paṭṭamaiyiṉ ivai nūṉmarapu paṟṟiya peyarāyiṉa eṉac civañāṉamuṉivarum ivviyaliṉ peyark kāraṇam kūṟiṉār. ivviyaluṭ kūṟappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyar mutaliyaṉa aṉaittul tolkāppiyaṉārkku muṟkālattavarāṉa paṇṭait tamiḻc cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa eḻuttiyal marapukaḷāy innūlil āciriyarāl eṭuttāḷap paṭṭaṉavām. eṉpa, pulavar, moḻipa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉṟāṅku muṉṉaiyōr karuttāka ivviyalil varuṅ kuṟiyīṭukaḷai āciriyar eṭutturaittalāl ivvuṇmai viḷaṅkum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇam moḻiyiṭai eḻuttiṟkaṉṟit taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkuriyatākum.
kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum yātāyiṉum oru collaic cārntuvariṉallatu taṉi yeḻuttāka olittu niṟkum iyalpuṭaiyaṉa alla. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉai naṉkaṟinta tamiḻ muṉṉōr immūṉṟiṉaiyum cārpeḻutteṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉmūṉṟu eṉavum, cārntu variṉallatu tamakkiyalpila veṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum eṉavum varun toṭarkaḷāl tolkāppiyaṉār ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉait teḷivāka viḷakkukiṉṟār.
taṉittiyaṅkum iyalpuṭaiyatu uyir. avviyalpiṉṟi uyiriṉāl iyakkappaṭuvatu mey. akara mutal aukāra mīṟāka vuḷḷa paṉṉiraṇṭeḻuttum taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaluṭaimai yāl uyireṉappaṭṭaṉa. kakara mutal ṉakara mīṟākavuḷḷa patiṉeṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷum taṉittiyaṅku miyalpiṉṟi akara mutaliya uyirkaḷāl iyakkappaṭutaliṉ meyyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. uyirvaḻi yāyaṭaṅki ataṉatu viḷakkam peṟṟu niṟkum uṭampaip pōṉṟu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum tammēl ēṟiya uyireḻuttiṉ māttiraikkuḷ aṭaṅki ataṉ oliyōṭu otticaippaṉavākum. iṅṅaṉam meyyum uyirum kūṭiyolikkum eḻuttoliyiṉai uyirmey eṉṟa niṟaiyuvamap peyarāl vaḻaṅkutal paḻaiya tamiḻ marapākum.
ivviyalil 33 cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eḻuttiṉṉa teṉpatum, ataṉ vakaiyum, eḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiraiyum, avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivam, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, uyir, mey, uyirmey, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭaiyeḻuttu, eṉa avai peṟum peyarkaḷum, mey taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum kūṭiyolikkum meymayakkamum, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ a i u eṉpavaṟṟukkuc cuṭṭeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ā ē ō eṉpavaṟṟukku viṉāveṉṉuṅ kuṟiyum, eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭolikku miṭamum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. 
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai 10, pak. 162-166




mutalāvatu
nūṉmarapu
1.		eḻutteṉappaṭupa
				akaramuta
				ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵ teṉpa
				cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
eṉpatu cūttiram.
ivvatikāram eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eḻuttilak kaṇam uṇarttiṉamai kāraṇattāṉ eḻuttatikārameṉṉum peyarttu. eḻuttai uṇarttiya atikāram eṉa virikka. atikāram-muṟaimai.
eḻuttu uṇarttumiṭattu eṉaittu vakaiyāṉ uṇartti ṉārō veṉiṉ, eṭṭuvakaiyāṉum, eṭṭiṟanta palavakaiyāṉum uṇarttiṉāreṉka.
1eṭṭuvakaiya eṉpār kūṟumāṟu:- eḻuttu iṉait teṉṟalum, iṉṉa peyariṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa muṟaiyiṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa aḷaviṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa piṟappiṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa puṇarcciyiṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa vaṭiviṉa veṉṟalum, iṉṉa taṉmaiyiṉa veṉṟalumām. ivaṟṟuḷ taṉmaiyum vaṭivum namakku uṇarttalākāmaiyiṉ āciriyar īṇṭu uraittilar. ēṉaiya itaṉuṭ peṟutum.
eḻuttu iṉaitteṉṟalait tokai vakai viriyāṉ uṇarka. muppattumūṉ ṟeṉpatu tokai. uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, uṭampu patiṉeṭṭuñ, cārpiṟṟōṟṟam mūṉṟum ataṉ vakai. aḷapeṭai yēḻum, uyirmey irunūṟṟorupattāṟum avaṟ ṟōṭuṅ kūṭṭi irunūṟṟaimpattāṟeṉal viri.
iṉi, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyum ic cūttirattāṟ peṟṟām. vakai: aukāra iṟuvāy (eḻu. 8) eṉpata ṉāṉum, ṉakāra iṟuvāy (eḻu. 9) eṉpataṉāṉum, avaitāṅ, kuṟṟiyalikaraṅkuṟṟiyalukaram’(eḻu. 2) eṉpataṉāṉum peṟṟām. viri: kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ 
(eḻu. 4) eṉpataṉāṉum, puḷḷi yillā (eḻu. 17)vṉpataṉāṉumbpaṟṟām.msî, avaṟṟuḷ, a i u (eḻu. 3) eṉpataṉāṉum, ā ī ū (eḻu. 4) eṉpataṉāṉum, meyyiṉaḷavē (eḻu. 11) eṉpataṉāṉum, avviyaṉilaiyum (eḻu. 12) eṉpataṉāṉum peṟṟām.
piṟappu, piṟappiyaluṭ peṟṟām.
puṇarcci, uyiriṟu  coṉmuṉ (eḻu. 107) eṉpataṉāṉum, avaṟṟu ṇiṟuttacolliṉ (eḻu. 108) eṉpataṉāṉum, piṟavāṟ ṟāṉum peṟṟām.
ïṉi, 2eṭṭiṟanta palvakaiya eṉpār kūṟumāṟu:- 
eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟaivuṅ, kūṭṭamum, pirivum, mayakkamum, moḻiyākkamum, nilaiyum, iṉamum, oṉṟupala vātalun, tirintataṉṟiripu atuveṉṟalum, piṟiteṉṟalum, atuvum piṟitumeṉṟalum, nilaiyiṟ ṟeṉṟalum, nilaiyāteṉṟalum, nilaiyiṟṟum nilaiyātumeṉṟalum, iṉṉōraṉṉa palavumām.
kuṟaivu, araiyaḷapu kuṟukal (eḻut. 13), ōraḷapākum (eḻut. 57) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ peṟṟām.
kūṭṭam, meyyō ṭiyaiyiṉum (eḻut. 10), puḷḷiyillā (eḻut. 17) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ peṟṟām.
pirivu, meyyuyir nīṅkiṉ (eḻut. 139) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.
mayakkam, ṭa ṟa la ḷa (eḻu. 23) eṉpatu mutalāka meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ (eḻut. 30) eṉpatu īṟākak kiṭantaṉa vaṟṟāṟ peṟṟām.
moḻiyākkam, ōreḻuttorumoḻi (eḻu. 45) eṉpata ṉāṟ peṟṟām, avveḻuttukkaḷai moḻiyākkaliṉ.
nilai, paṉṉīruyirum (eḻu. 59), uyirmeyyallaṉa (eḻu. 60), uyir au (eḻu. 69), ñaṇanamaṉa (eḻu. 78) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟāṉ moḻikku mutalām eḻuttum īṟāmeḻuttum peṟṟām.
iṉam, valleḻutteṉpa (eḻu. 19), melleḻut teṉpa (eḻut. 20), iṭaiyeḻutteṉpa (eḻu. 21), aukāra iṟuvāy    (eḻut. 8), ṉakāra iṟuvāy (eḻut. 9) eṉpaṉa vaṟṟāṟ peṟṟām. ivaṟṟāṉē eḻuttukaḷ uruvātalum peṟṟām. ivvuruvākiya ōcaikku āciriyar vaṭivu kūṟāmai uṇarka. iṉi varivaṭivu kūṟuṅkāl meykkē perumpāṉmai yum vaṭivu kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.
oṉṟu palavātal, eḻuttōraṉṉa (eḻut. 141) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.
tirintataṉṟiripatuveṉṟal, takaram varuvaḻi (eḻut. 369) eṉpataṉāṉum, piṟāṇṭum peṟṟām.
piṟiteṉṟal, makara iṟuti (eḻut. 310), ṉakāra iṟuti . 332) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṟ peṟṟām.
atuvum piṟitumeṉṟal, āṟaṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi (eḻut. 115) eṉpataṉāṟpeṟṟām.
nilaiyiṟṟeṉṟal, niṟutta colli ṉīṟāku (eḻut. 108) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.
nilaiyāteṉṟal, nilaimoḻiyatu īṟṟukkaṇṇiṉṟum varumoḻiyatu mutaṟkaṇṇiṉṟum puṇarcci tammuḷ ilavātal. atu maruviṉ ṟokuti (eḻut. 111) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.
nilaiyiṟṟum nilaiyātu meṉṟal, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum (eḻut. 226) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya akaram irāveṉ kiḷavikkakaramillai (eḻut. 227) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām.
ikkūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ karuviyuñ ceykaiyu meṉa iru vakaiya.
avaṟṟuṭ karuvi, puṟappuṟak karuviyum, puṟak karuviyum, akappuṟak karuviyum, akakkaruviyu meṉa nālvakaittu. nūṉmarapum piṟappiyalum puṟappuṟak karuvi. moḻimarapu puṟakkaruvi. puṇariyal akappuṟak karuvi. ekara okaram peyark kīṟākā (eḻut. 272) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa akakkaruvi.
iṉic ceykaiyum, puṟappuṟacceykaiyum, puṟacceykai yum, akappuṟacceykaiyum, akacceykaiyumeṉa nālvakaittu. ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē (eḻut. 140) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa puṟappuṟac ceykai. laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar (eḻut. 149) eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa puṟacceykai. ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti (eḻut. 163) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa akappuṟac ceykai. tokaimarapu mutaliya ōttiṉuḷ iṉṉa īṟu iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉac ceykai kūṟuvaṉa vellām akac ceykai. ivvikaṟpa mellān tokaiyāka uṇarka.
ivvōt teṉṉutaliṟṟō eṉiṉ, atuvum ataṉ peyar uraippavē aṭaṅkum.
ivvōt teṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, ittol kāppiyameṉṉum nūṟku marapān tuṇaikku vēṇṭuvaṉa vaṟṟait tokuttu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ nūṉmarapeṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
nūleṉṟatu nūlpōṟaliṉ oppiṉāyatōr ākupeya rām. avvoppāyavā ṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, kuṟṟaṅ kaḷaintu eḵkiya paṉṉuṉaip pañcikaḷaiyellāṅ kaivaṉ makaṭūut tūymaiyum nuṇmaiyu muṭaiyavāka ōriḻaip paṭuttiṉāṟ pōla . ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiva ṉālē (marapu. 94) vaḻukkaḷaintu eḵkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai yellām mutalum muṭivum māṟu kōḷiṉṟākavun tokaiyiṉum vakaiyiṉum poruṇmai kāṭṭi yum, uraiyuṅ kāṇṭikaiyum uḷniṉṟu akalavum, īraiṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟi īraintaḻaku peṟa muppatti raṇṭu tantiravuttiyoṭu puṇaravum,
xruporu ṇutaliya cūttirat tāṉu
midbkhÊ kiḷanta vōtti ṉāṉum
potumoḻi kiḷanta paṭalat tāṉu
mūṉṟuṟup paṭakkiya piṇṭat tāṉum				(cey. 166)
oru neṟippaṭap puṇarkkappaṭūun taṉmai yuṭaimaiyā ṉeṉka. marapu, ilakkaṇam, muṟaimai, taṉmai eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ kiḷavi.
āyiṉ nūleṉṟatu īṇṭu mūṉṟatikārattiṉaiyu maṉṟē? ivvōttu mūṉṟatikārattiṟkum ilakkaṇamāyavā ṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu peyarum muṟaiyum ivvatikārat tiṟkuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟkum oppak kūṟiyatu. īṇṭuk kūṟiya muppattu mūṉṟaṉaip patiṉaintākki āṇṭut tokai kōṭalil tokai vēṟām. aḷavu, ceyyuḷiyaṟkum ivvatikārattiṟkum otta aḷavum ovvā aḷavu muḷavākak kūṟiyatu. kuṟiṟkum neṭiṟkuṅ kūṟiya māttirai iraṇṭiṭattiṟkum otta aḷavu. āṇṭuk kūṟuñ ceyyuṭku aḷapu kōṭaṟku īṇṭaikkup payaṉ tārāta aḷapeṭai kūṟiyatu ovvā aḷavu. aḵtu, aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum (eḻut.33) eṉṉuñ cūttirattōṭu āṇṭu māṭṭeṟiyumāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉṉuṅ kuṟilum neṭilum mūvakaiyiṉamum āytamum vaṇṇattiṟkum ivvatikārat tiṟkum oppak kūṟiyaṉa. kuṟaivum iraṇṭaṟkum okkum. kūṭṭamum pirivum mayakkamum ivvatikārattiṟkē uriyaṉavākak kūṟiyaṉa. ammūvāṟum (eḻut. 22) eṉṉuñ cūttiram mutaliyaṉavaṟṟāṉ eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic collāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟamaiyiṟ collatikārattiṟkum ilakkaṇam īṇṭuk kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam mūṉṟatikārattiṟkum ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟutaliṉ ivvōttu nūliṉatu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. nūleṉṟatu tolkāppiya meṉṉum piṇṭattai. iv vōttilakkaṇaṅkaṅkaṭām eḻuttukkaḷatu peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyum aḷavuṅ kuṟaivuṅ kūṭṭamum pirivum mayakkamum ām. ēṉaiya ivvatikārattuḷ ēṉaiyōttukkaḷuḷ uṇarttupa.
aṟṟēl aḵtāka; ittalaiccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, eḻuttukaḷatu peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum uṇarttuta ṉutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: eḻutteṉappaṭupa - eḻutteṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭuvaṉa, akaramutal ṉakaraiṟuvāy muppaḵ teṉpa - akaram mutal ṉakaram īṟākak kiṭanta muppateṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē - cārntu varutalait tamakku ilakkaṇamāka vuṭaiya mūṉṟum allāta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ammūṉṟuṅ kūṭiyavaḻi muppattu mūṉṟeṉpa. 
a - ā - i - ī - u - ū - e - ē - ai - o - ō - au - k - ṅ - c - ñ - ṭ - ṇ - t - n - p - m - y - r - l - v - ḻ - ḷ - ṟ - ṉ eṉavarum. eṉappaṭupa veṉṟu ciṟappittuṇarttutalāṉ aḷapeṭaiyum uyirmeyyum ittuṇaic ciṟappila. ōcaiyuṇarvārkkuk karuviyākiya varivaṭivuñ ciṟappilā eḻuttākak koḷḷappaṭum.
akaram mutalātal āriyattiṟkum okkumēṉum īṇṭut tamiḻeḻuttē kūṟukiṉṟāreṉpatu uṇartaṟku ṉakara viṟuvāy yeṉṟār.
paṭupa, paṭuva; paṭupa veṉpatu paṭuttalōcaiyāl toḻiṟ peyarākak kūṟappaṭum. pakaramum vakaramum īṇṭu niṟṟaṟkut tammuḷ otta urimaiyavēṉum eḻutteṉappaṭuva veṉat tūkkaṟṟu niṟkuñ coṟcīraṭikkup paṭupaveṉpatu iṉṉōcaittāy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭup paṭupa veṉṟē pāṭam ōtuka. iḵtu aṉ peṟāta akaravīṟṟup palavaṟicol.
akara ṉakara meṉavē peyaruṅ kūṟiṉār. 
eḻuttukaṭ kellām akaram mutalātaṟkuk kāraṇam meyyi ṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum (eḻut. 46) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟupa. vīṭupēṟṟiṟku uriya āṇmakaṉai uṇarttuñ ciṟappāṉ ṉakaram piṉ vaittār. iṉi eḻuttukaṭkuk kiṭakkaimuṟai yāyiṉavāṟu kūṟutum. 
kuṟṟeḻuttukaḷai muṉṉākak kūṟi avaṟṟiṟku iṉa motta neṭṭeḻuttukaḷai avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉākak kūṟiṉār, oru māttirai kūṟiyē iraṇṭu māttirai kūṟavēṇṭutaliṉ. aṉṟi iraṇṭai muṟkūṟiṉālō veṉiṉ, ākātu; oṉṟu niṉṟu ataṉōṭu piṉṉarum oṉṟu kūṭiyē iraṇṭāvataṉṟi iraṇṭeṉpa toṉṟu iṉṟātaliṉ. itaṉāṉ oṉṟutāṉ pala kūṭiyē eṇ virintateṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaram eṇṇum piṟappum poruḷum ottaliṉ vaittār. ikarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaram vaittār, piṟappu ovvātēṉum a - i - u ammūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu (eḻut. 31) eṉac cuṭṭup poruṭṭāy niṟkiṉṟa iṉaṅ karuti. avai aimpāṟ kaṇṇum perumpāṉmai varumāṟu uṇarka. ekaram ataṉpiṉ vaittār, akara ikaraṅka ḷoṭu piṟappu oppumai paṟṟi. aikāra aukāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttu ilavēṉum piṟappu oppumai paṟṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar aikāra aukāram vaittār. okaram no eṉa meyyoṭu kūṭi niṉṟallatu tāṉāka ōreḻuttoru moḻi yākāta ciṟappiṉmai nōkki aikārattiṉ piṉ vaittār. a i u e eṉṉum nāṉkum akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ ekkoṟṟaṉ eṉa, meyyoṭu kūṭāmal tām iṭaiccollāy niṉṟāyiṉum mēl varum peyarkaḷōṭu kūṭic cuṭṭup poruḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttum. okaram meyyoṭu kūṭiyē taṉ poruḷ uṇarttuvatallatu tāṉākap poruḷuṇarttā teṉṟu uṇarka. iṉṉum a - ā - u - ū - e - ē - o - ō - au eṉpaṉa tammuḷ vaṭivu okkum. i ī ai tammuḷ vaṭivu ovvā. iṉṉum ivai aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl neṭṭeḻuttōṭu kuṟṟeḻuttukku ōcai iyaiyumāṟṟāṉum uṇarka. iṉic cuṭṭu nīṇṭu ākāra īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷāta lāṉum poruḷ okkum. puṇarcci oppumai uyir mayaṅkiyaluṭ peṟutum. immuṟai vaḻuvāmal mēl āḷumāṟu uṇarka.
iṉik kakāra ṅakāramuñ cakāra ñakāramum ṭakāra ṇakāramun takāra nakāramum pakāra makāramun tamakkup piṟappuñ ceykaiyum ottaliṉ, valloṟṟiṭaiyē melloṟṟuk kalantu vaittār. mutaṉāvum mutalaṇṇamum iṭaināvum iṭaiyaṇṇamum nuṉināvum nuṉiyaṇṇamum  itaḻiyaitalu mākip piṟakkiṉṟa iṭattiṉ muṟaimai nōkki av veḻuttukkaḷaik ka ca ṭa ta pa, ṅa ña ṇa na ma veṉa immuṟaiyē vaittār. piṟappu oppumaiyāṉum ṉakāram ṟakāramāyt tirita lāṉum ṟakāramum ṉakāramuñ cēravaittār. ivai tamiḻeḻut teṉpatu aṟivittaṟkup piṉṉar vaittār. iṉi iṭai yeḻuttukkaḷil yakāram muṉvaittār, atuvum uyirkaḷ pōla miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷi aṇṇaṅkaṇṇuṟṟu aṭaiyap piṟattaliṉ. rakāram ataṉoṭu piṟappu ovvātēṉuñ ceykai ottaliṉ ataṉ piṉ vaittār. lakāramum vakāramun tammiṟ piṟappuñ ceykaiyum ovvāvēṉuṅ, kal valitu col valitu eṉṟāṟ pōlat tammiṟ cērntuvaruñ coṟkaḷ perumpāṉmai yeṉpatu paṟṟi lakāramum vakāramuñ cēra vaittār. ḻakāramum ḷakāramum oṉṟāṉum iyaipilavēṉum iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa  (eḻut. 21)  eṉṟāṟ canta viṉpattiṟku iyaipuṭaimai karutic cēra vaittār pōlum.
akaram uyirakaramum uyirmeyyakaramu meṉa iraṇṭu. iḵtu ēṉai yuyirkaṭkum okkum. eṉavē, ōruyir patiṉeṭṭā yiṟṟu.
ivveḻutteṉappaṭṭa ōcaiyai aruveṉpār aṟiyā tār. ataṉai uruveṉṟē kōṭum. atu ceṟippac cēṟa lāṉuñ, ceṟippa varutalāṉum, iṭai yeṟiyappaṭutalāṉuñ, cevikkaṭ ceṉṟu uṟuta lāṉum, iṉpatuṉpattai ākkuta lāṉum, uruvum uruvuṅkūṭip piṟattalāṉun, talaiyum miṭaṟum neñcumeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum nilaipeṟṟup pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uṟappiṟakku meṉṟamaiyāṉum uruvēyām. aruvē yāyiṉ ivviṭattiṟ kūṟiyaṉa iṉmai uṇarka. allatūum, vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai yeṉṟu ōtiṉamaiyāṉum uṇarka. uṭampoṭu puṇarttaleṉṉum ilakkaṇattāṉ ivvōcai uruvātal nilaipeṟṟateṉṟu uṇarka. ataṟkuk kāraṇamum muṉṉark kūṟiṉām.
ivveḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruviṟku vaṭivu kūṟārāyiṉar, atu muppattiraṇṭu vaṭiviṉuḷ iṉṉa eḻuttiṟku iṉṉa vaṭiveṉap piṟarkku uṇarttutaṟku ariteṉpatu karuti. avvaṭivu ārāyu miṭattup peṟṟapeṟṟa vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivām; kuḻalakattiṟ kūṟiṟ kuḻalvaṭivuṅ, kuṭattakattiṟ kūṟiṟ kuṭavaṭivum, veḷḷiṭaiyiṟ kūṟiṉ ellāt ticaiyum nīrttaraṅkamum pōla.
ellā meyyu muruvuru vāki (eḻut. 17) eṉavum, uṭ peṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē (eḻut. 14) eṉavum, meyyiṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal (eḻut. 15) eṉavuñ ciṟupāṉmai vaṭivuṅ kūṟuvar. atu vaṭṭañ caturam mutaliya muppattiraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟai uṇarttum. maṉattāṉ uṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvu il lōrum uṇartaṟku eḻuttukkaṭku vēṟu vēṟu vaṭivaṅkāṭṭi eḻutappaṭṭu naṭattaliṟ kaṭpulaṉākiya varivaṭivum uṭaiya vāyiṉa. perumpāṉmai meykkē vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. uyirkku vaṭiviṉmaiyiṉ ekaravekarat tiyaṟkaiyu maṟṟē (eḻut. 16) eṉac ciṟupāṉmai uyirkkum vaṭivu kūṟiṉār.	(1)
kaṇēcayyar kuṟippu:
iṉaittu - ivvaḷaviṟṟu. vaṭivu eṉṟatu olivaṭivai. kuṟaivu eṉṟatu māttiraik kuṟukkattai. kūṭṭam - uyirum meyyum kūṭal. pirivu - kūṭiya uyirum meyyum pirintu niṟṟal. oṉṟu palavātal - ōreḻuttāy niṉṟaṉa pirintu palavātal. aḵtāvatu, `kuṉṟēṟāmā' eṉpuḻi māviṉ muṉ niṉṟa `ṟā' eṉṉum ōreḻuttē. `kuṉṟēṟu āmā' eṉap pirippuḻi `ṟu-ā' eṉap palavātal. itaṉai, ivvatikārattu 141ām cūttirattā laṟika.
tirintataṉ ṟiripu atuveṉṟalāvatu nilaimoḻi varu moḻikaḷil yāṅkāyiṉum ōreḻuttiṟkut tiripu kūṟi ammoḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku, mīḷavun tiripu kūṟuṅkāl attiripeḻuttu (tiriyappaṭṭa eḻuttu) moḻiyai yeṭuttuk kūṟāmal tiriyappaṭṭaveḻuttu moḻi at tirinta eḻuttu moḻiyumām eṉṉum nayampaṟṟi at tirinta eḻuttu moḻi yaiyē yeṭuttup puṇarccikūṟal. utāraṇam urupiyalil, `nīye ṉorupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum āvayi ṉakara moṟṟākummē' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl `nī' `niṉ' eṉat tiriyumeṉak kūṟi, mīḷavum anta `nī' eṉpataṟkup poruṭ puṇarcci viti kūṟuṅkāl, muṟkūṟiya `niṉ'  eṉṉum tiripu moḻiyai yeṭuttuk kūṟāmal, `niṉ' eṉat tirintatum `nīyē' eṉpatu paṟṟi ataṉaiyēyeṭuttu `nīye ṉorupeya rurupiya ṉilaiyum' eṉak kūṟal kāṇka. iṉṉum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil `mūṉṟu māṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum' eṉṟa āciriyar. `mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē pakāramākum' eṉak kūṟutalum, `āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutaṉīṭummē, eṉak kūṟutalum aṉṉa vātal uṇarka. iṉṉum, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalil, lakaravīṟṟu moḻikaṭkut tiripu kūṟuṅkāl' `alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa' eṉak kūṟi lakaram ṟakaramākumeṉak kūṟiya āciriyar mīḷavum avaṟṟiṟkut tiripu kūṟuṅkāl, `takaram varumvaḻi yāytam nilaiyalum' eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl lakaram āytamākat tiriyumeṉṟu tirinta moḻi yīṟṟaiyē (lakarattaiyē) eṭuttuk kūṟalumatuvām. iṉi la ṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉart tanaveṉavariṟ ṟaṉavākummē', eṉpataṉāl takaram ṟakaramākat tiriyumeṉṟu kūṟiya āciriyar, mīḷa lakarattiṟkut tiripu kūṟuṅkāl ṟakarameṉat tiripeḻuttu moḻiyai eṭuttuk kūṟāmal `takaram varumvaḻi' eṉat tirinta eḻuttu moḻiyai yeṭuttuk kūṟalum atuvēyām. naṉṉūluraiyuḷ mayilainātar  `tirintataṉ ṟiripu matuvumā morōvaḻi' eṉṟu kūṟaliṉ it tirintataṉ ṟiripu atuveṉṟaṉ mutaliyaṉa muṉ yātō oru nūlilē cūttiraṅkaḷā yiruntaṉapōlum.
tirintataṉ ṟiripu piṟiteṉṟalāvatu  ōrīṟu piṟitōrīṟākat tirintu avvāṟē (piṟitīṟākavē) niṉṟu puṇarumeṉṟal. atu marameṉpataṉōṭu aṭiyeṉṉum varumoḻi puṇaruṅkāl makaraṅkeṭṭu akaravīṟākavē (piṟitīṟākavē) niṉṟu puṇarutal pōlvatu. iṅṅaṉamē `ṉakaravīṟu'm ṟakaramākat tirintu piṟi tīṟākavē niṉṟu puṇartal kāṇka. 
tirintataṉ tiripu atuvum piṟitumeṉṟalāvatu - ōrīṟuvē ṟōreḻuttup peṟṟu piṟitīṟāka niṉṟu puṇarumeṉak kūṟi, attiripīṟṟiṟkāyiṉum attiripīṟṟōṭu puṇarum varumoḻik kāyiṉum mīḷavum oṉṟu vitikkavēṇṭi, attiripīṟṟai eṭuttup puṇarkkuṅkāl, attiripīṟu tirinta īṟṟōṭuṅ (iyal pīṟṟōṭum) kūṭi niṟṟaliṉ, atuvum (iyalpīṟum) piṟitum (vitiyīṟum) āyē niṉṟu puṇarumeṉṟal. atu, āṟaṉurupōṭum nāṉkaṉuru pōṭum puṇaruṅkāl neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟa tam, nam, em, taṉ, niṉ, eṉ eṉṉum moḻikaḷiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa meykaḷ ōrakaram peṟṟup puṇarumeṉa, "āṟaṉurupiṉu nāṉka ṉurupiṉuṅ - kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭalillai - yīṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu nilaiyu - neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa" eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl vititta āciriyar mīḷa avvakaram peṟṟa tam mutaliya moḻikaḷōṭu atu urupai vaittup puṇarttuṅ kāl, ammoḻikaḷai makaram mutaliya puḷḷi yīṟum (atuvum) akaravīṟum (piṟitum) ākiya iraṇṭīṟumāyē, "āṟaṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi yīṟākakara muṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum" eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ, īṟākakaram eṉa vaittup puṇarttal kāṇka. īṟākakaram - puḷḷiyīṟṟukkaḷukku īṟākiya akaram eṉpatu poruḷ. tam mutaliya eṉṉātu tāmutaliya puḷḷiyīṟṟakara meṉṟu puḷḷiyīṟu kūṟiṉamai yiṉāṉē atuvum. avaṟṟiṉ īṟākiya akaram eṉṟataṉāṉē piṟitun tōṉṟak kūṟiṉamē kāṇka. iṉṉum `verine ṉiṟuti muḻutuṅ keṭuvaḻi - varumiṭa ṉuṭaittē melleḻut tiyaṟkai' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `verinu' eṉṟu collavēṇṭiyatai `verin' eṉṟataṉāṉē tirintataṉ ṟiripu atu veṉpatum.  `muḻutum keṭuvaḻi' eṉṟataṉālē piṟi teṉpatum tōṉṟak kūṟiyamaiyāṉē atuvum tirintataṉtiripu atuvum piṟitum eṉṟalām. iṉṉum, it tiripukaḷ piṟavāṟu varuvaṉa vuḷavēṉu maṟintukoḷka.
nilaiyiṟṟeṉṟal eṉṟatu - nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻi yum poruṭporuttamuṟap puṇarum taḻuvutoṭarp puṇarcci vitikaḷai. `poṟkuṭam' eṉpatupōla varun toṭar moḻi kaḷil nilai moḻiyum varumoḻiyum poruṭ poruttamuṟa oṉṟai oṉṟu taḻuvip puṇarntu niṟṟaliṉ āṇṭuk kūṟuñ ceykai eṉṟum nilaiyuṭaimaiyiṉ nilaiyiṟṟeṉṟal eṉṟār. pataccēta kālattun nilaittalāṉē nilaiyiṟṟeṉṟār.
nilaiyāteṉṟal eṉṟatu - nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻi yum poruṭporuttamuṟap puṇarāta taḻāat toṭarp puṇarcci vitikaḷai. muṉṟil eṉpatupōla varum toṭar moḻikaḷil nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum poruṭ poruttamuṟa oṉṟai oṉṟu taḻuvip puṇarntu nillāmaiyiṉ (muṉṟil eṉpatu il muṉ eṉap poruḷ koḷḷuṅkāl nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻi yun tammuḷ iyaiyāmaiyiṉ) āṇṭuk kūṟuñ ceykai eṉṟum nilaiyāmaiyiṉ nilaiyā teṉṟal eṉṟār. "māyiru maruppiṟ paralava laṭaiya iralai teṟippa" eṉpatilum maruppiṉ eṉpatu paralōṭiyai yātu iralaiyōṭiyaitaliṉ aṅṅaṉam iyaiyuṅ kāl āṇṭu ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tirinta ceykai nilai peṟā maiyiṉ (ilavātaliṉ) atupōlvaṉavum aṉṉavē yām. itaṉai "maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyu - muriyavai yuḷavē puṇar nilaic cuṭṭē" eṉṉum (eḻu - 111) cūttira urai nōkki uṇarka. pataccēta kālattum annuvaya kālattum nilaiyā maiyāṉē nilaiyāteṉṟār.
nilaiyiṟṟum nilaiyātumeṉṟal - ōriṭattil peṟṟa puṇarcci nilai atupōṉṟa vēṟōriṭattil nilaipeṟā teṉṟal. atu `palāakkōṭu' eṉak kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa ākāravīṟu peṟṟa akarattai atupōṉṟa irāveṉkiḷavi peṟāteṉa vilakkal pōlvaṉa. kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa ākāra vīṟu akaram peṟum eṉṟa ippuṇarcci viti palā mutaliya ākāra vīṟṟil nilaipeṟṟum atuvē īṟāya irāveṉ kiḷaviyil nilaipeṟātum varutaliṉ nilaiyiṟṟum nilaiyātumeṉṟal eṉṟār.
karuvi
karuvi - ceykaikkuriya karuvi. ikkaruvi ceykaikku nērē karuviyāvatum paramparaiyāṟ karuviyāvatum eṉa iruvakaittu. akak karuviyum akappuṟakkaruviyum nērē karuviyāvaṉa. ēṉaiya paramparaiyāṟ karuviyāvaṉa. nūṉmarapu piṟappiyalkaḷiṟ kūṟum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum coṟkuk karuviyākumukattāl ceykaikkuk karuviyātaliṉ paramparaiyāṟ karuviyāyiṉa. 
akakkaruviyāvatu - ceykaippaṭutaṟkuriya nilaimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttup paṟṟi varum vitikaḷaik kūṟuvatu. aḵtu "ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā" eṉṟāṟ pōlvatu. itu ceykaik kuriya īṟṟeḻuttuppaṟṟiya vitiyātaliṉ akakkaruviyāyiṟṟu.
"aḷaviṟku niṟaiyiṟku moḻimutalāki - yuḷaveṉappaṭṭa voṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē - yavaitāṅ - ka ca ta pa veṉṟā na ma va veṉṟā - akara ukaramō ṭavaiyeṉa moḻipa" eṉpatum akak karuviyākum; ceykaikku aṇṇiya karuviyātaliṉ, mutaṉilai iṟutinilaikaḷum akakkaruviyākum; moḻikku mutaṉiṟkum eḻuttukkaḷum īṟṟiṉiṟku meḻuttukkaḷuñ ceykaikku pakārappaṭutaliṉ. 
akappuṟakkaruviyāvatu  -puṇarcci ilakkaṇamum puṇarccik kuriya tiripukaḷ ivaiyeṉpatum, iyalpum. puṇarcci vakaiyum, nilaimoḻikaḷ ceykai vitiyiṟ peṟuñ cāriyaikaḷ varumoḻiyoṭu puṇaruṅkā laṭaiyun tiripukaḷu māki irumoḻikaḷum ceykaippaṭutaṟ kēṟṟavāyvarum vitikaḷaik kūṟuvatu.
puṟakkaruviyāvatu - ceykaikkuriya nilaimoḻi varumoḻikaḷāy niṟkumoḻikaḷiṉ marapu kūṟuvatu. atu moḻimarapu. atu ceykaikkuriya karuvi viti kūṟātu ceykaip paṭutaṟkuriya moḻikaḷiṉ marapu kūṟutaliṉ puṟakkaruviyāyiṟṟu.
puṟappuṟakkaruviyāvatu - moḻikaḷātaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷatu ilakkaṇamum piṟappuṅ kūṟuvatu. atu nūṉmarapum piṟappiyalumām. avai ceykaikkuriya puṟakkaruvi yākiya moḻikaḷātaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum piṟappuṅ kūṟutaliṉ puṟappuṟak karuvi yāyiṉa. iṅṅaṉamē nālvakaik karuviyiṉ ilakkaṇamumaṟintu koḷka.
eḻuttukkaḷ moḻiyāki niṉṟu piṉ ceykai aṭaitaliṉ avaṟṟiṉilakkaṇaṅkaḷaic ceykaikkup puṟappuṟak karuvi yeṉṟum. ammoḻikaḷē, nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyāka niṉṟu ceykai peṟutaliṉ moḻikaḷiṉi lakkaṇaṅkaḷaip puṟakkaruvi yeṉṟum, aṅṅaṉam moḻikaḷ puṇaruṅkāl nilai moḻiyīṟum varumoḻi mutalumaṭaikiṉṟa tiripilakkaṇaṅ kaḷaiyum iyalpaiyum, nilaimoḻi peṟuñ cāriyaikaḷ ivai eṉpataiyum. avaṟṟiṉ tiripu mutaliyavaṟṟai yum kūṟutaliṉ puṇariyalai akap puṟak karuviyeṉṟum, nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil niṟṟaṟkuriya eḻuttu viti mutaliyavaikaḷaik kūṟum vitikaḷai akakkaruviyeṉṟum vakuttaṉar eṉka. ivaṟṟuḷ eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamum moḻi yiṉilakkaṇa mum paramparaiyāṟ karuviyātal kāṇka. karuvi yoṉṟē akam, akappuṟam, puṟam, puṟappuṟam eṉac ceykai nōkki nāṉkāka vakukkappaṭṭatu.
ceykai
iṉic ceykai nāṉkaṉuḷ akacceykaiyāvatu - nilai moḻiyīṟu iṉṉa iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉak kūṟuvatu. atu poṟkuṭam eṉṟāṟpōlvatu. itu īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ paṭuñ ceykai vitiyātaliṉ akamāyiṟṟu. itupaṟṟiyē tokai marapu mutaliya ōttiṉuḷ iṉṉaīṟu iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉac ceykai kūṟuvaṉa vellāmakacceykaiyeṉṟār uraikārar. ceykai yōttukkaḷai akattōtteṉpatum itupaṟṟiyē yām. 
akappuṟacceykaiyāvatu nilaimoḻiyīṟu peṟum muṭi paṉṟi nilaimoḻiyīṟu peṟṟu varum eḻuttu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu kūṟuvatu. atu puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ ukaram peṟumeṉa vititta puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ piṉ avvukaram peṟāveṉa vilakkutal pōlvaṉa. itu īṟṟeḻuttiṉ vitiyaṉṟi īṟṟeḻuttuppeṟṟu varum eḻuttaip paṟṟiya vitiyātaliṉ akappuṟamāyiṟṟu. itupaṟṟiyē "ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti - ekaramu muyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟkai" eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa. akappuṟac ceykai eṉṟār uraikārar. "vēṟṟumaik kukkeṭa vakaram nilaiyum" eṉavum "irāveṉkiḷavik kakara millai" eṉavum varuvaṉavumavai.
puṟacceykaiyāvatu varumoḻicceykai kūṟuvatu. itu nilaimoḻic ceykaiyaṉṟi varumoḻic ceykaiyātaliṉ puṟac ceykaiyāyiṟṟu. atu poṉṉaritu. poṉṟītu eṉṟāṟpōla varuvatu itupaṟṟiyē "laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉart - tanaveṉa variṟ ṟaṉavā kummē" eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa puṟacceykai yeṉṟār uraikārar. 
puṟappuṟacceykaiyāvatu - nilaimoḻiyīṟum varumoḻi mutalum ceykai peṟātu niṟpa avviraṇṭaiyum poruntutaṟku iṭaiyil uṭampaṭumey pōṉṟa ōreḻuttu varuvatu pōlvatu. atu `tīyaḻakitu' eṉṟāṟ pōlvatu. itupaṟṟiyē "ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē yuṭampaṭu meyyi ṉuruvu koḷal varaiyār" eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa puṟappuṟacceykai yeṉṟārurai kārar.  
nilaimoḻi yīṟupaṟṟi varuvatai akac ceykai yeṉṟum, avvīṟupaṟṟātu avvīṟu peṟṟuvarum eḻuttaip paṟṟi varuvatu avvīṟṟukkup puṟamātaliṉ ataṉai akap puṟameṉṟum. varu moḻic ceykaipaṟṟi varuñceykai nilaimoḻiyīṟum. atu peṟṟuvarum eḻuttum paṟṟivaruñ ceykaiyaṉṟi, avaṟṟiṟkup puṟamātaliṉ ataṉaip puṟac ceykai yeṉṟum, nilaimoḻi yīṟum varumoḻi mutalum paṟṟātu varuñ ceykai avviraṇṭaṟkum puṟamātaliṉ ataṉaip puṟappuṟameṉṟum kūṟiṉar eṉka. 
ku-pu: iṅkē kūṟiya karuviyuñ ceykaiyum eḻuttikārat tukku māttiramē uraiyāciriyarālum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarālum uraikkappaṭṭamaiyai avviruvaruraiyumnōkkit teḷintu koḷka.  
eḵkutal - pañciṉai noytākkal. mūṉṟuṟuppu - cūttiram. ottu, paṭalam. āṇṭu eṉṟatu  - ceyyuḷiyalai. vaṇṇam eṉṟatu ceyyuḷukkuriya vaṇṇaṅkaḷai. avai pāvaṇṇam mutaliyaṉa. ataṉaip poruḷatikāram 526-m cūttiram mutaliyavaṟṟāṉu ṇarntukoḷka.
uṭampoṭu puṇarttaleṉṉu milakkaṇattāliv vōcai yuruvāta ṉilaipeṟṟateṉpatu - paḻaṉimalaiyili rukkuṅ kumaraṉ tirupparaṅkuṉṟam tiruccentūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilu miruppā ṉeṉṟāl, paḻaṉimalaiyum avaṉukku ōriṭamātal tōṉṟutal pōla, eḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappiṭaṅ kaḷum vaṉmai meṉmaikaḷuñ colli ataṉkaṇ uruvuṭaiya veṉpataiyum peṟa vaitta maiyai. 
2.	avaitāṅ
		kuṟṟiya likaraṅ kuṟṟiya lukara
		māytameṉṟa
		muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa.
itu, mēṟ cārntuvarumeṉṟa mūṉṟiṟkum peyarum muṟai yum uṇarttuta ṉutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaitām - mēṟ cārntu varumeṉap paṭṭavaitām, kuṟṟiyalikaraṅ kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum - kuṟṟiyalikaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaramum āytamu meṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatākiya puḷḷi vaṭivumām; eḻuttōraṉṉa - avaiyum muṟkūṟiya muppateḻut tōṭu oru taṉmaiyavāy vaḻaṅkum e -ṟu.
muṟkūṟiya 3iraṇṭum ummai tokkuniṉṟaṉa. ikara ukaraṅ kuṟuki niṉṟaṉa, vikāravakaiyāṟ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutaliṉ. ivaṟṟai iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal ellārkkum oppa muṭintatu. cantaṉakkōl kuṟukiṉāṟ pirappaṅkōlākātu atu pōla uyiratu kuṟukkamum uyirēyām. ivaṟṟaip puṇarcci vēṟṟumaiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyum paṟṟi vēṟōr eḻuttāka vēṇṭiṉār.
ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram nērpacaiyum niraipacaiyumākac cīrkaḷaip palavākkumāṟu ceyyuḷiyaluḷ uṇarka.
āytameṉṟa ōcaitāṉ aṭuppukkūṭṭup pōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytameṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meṉṟār. ataṉai ikkālattār naṭuvu vāṅkiyiṭ ṭeḻutupa. itaṟku vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. ēṉai oṟṟukkaḷ pōla uyirēṟātu ōcaivikāramāy niṟpatoṉṟākaliṉ, eḻuttiyal taḻā ōcaikaḷ pōlak koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟaṟku eḻuttēyā meṉṟār. itaṉaip puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉavē, ēṉai eḻuttukkaḷellām varivaṭiviṉavātal peṟṟām.
muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṟ cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppaḵteṉpa eṉavē, cārntu varaṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟumē ciṟantaṉa, ēṉaiya muppatum avvāṟu ciṟantila veṉavum poruṭantu niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉai vilakkic ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu ivaiyum oppa vaḻaṅkumeṉṟaṟku eḻuttō raṉṉa eṉṟār.
ippeyarkaḷē peyar. immuṟaiyē muṟai. tokaiyum mūṉṟē. immūṉṟu peyarum paṇputtokai.
avaitām, āyta meṉṟa eṉpaṉa coṟcīraṭi.	(2)
3.	avaṟṟuḷ, 		a i u
			e o eṉṉu mappā laintu
			mōraḷa picaikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻut teṉpa.
itu, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku aḷavuṅ kuṟiyum uṇarttuta ṉutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya muppa teḻuttiṉuḷ, a i u e o eṉṉum appālaintum - akara ikara ukara ekara okaram eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum appakutikaḷaintum, ōraḷapu icaikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻutteṉpa - orōvoṉṟu ōraḷapāka olikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
ikkāraṇappeyar mēl āḷumāṟu āṇṭu uṇarka.
4tamakku iṉamāyavaṟṟiṉkaṇallatu kuṟumai neṭumai koḷḷap paṭāmaiyiṉ, aḷaviṟpaṭṭu amaintaṉa vāṅ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟ kuṟuki mey araimāttirai peṟṟatēṉuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉap peyar peṟātāyiṟṟu, oru māttirai peṟṟa mey taṉakku iṉamāka iṉmaiyiṉ. kuṟṟeḻut teṉpatu paṇput tokai.
5iṉi icaippatum icaiyum vēṟāka uṇaraṟka, atu poruṭ ṭaṉmai.
avaṟṟuḷ,  a i u eṉpaṉa coṟcīraṭi.	(3)
4.	ā ī ū 		ē ai
			ō au eṉṉu mappā lēḻu
			mīraḷa picaikkum neṭṭeḻut teṉpa.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ā ī ū ē ai ō au eṉṉum appāl ēḻum - ā ī ū ē ai ō au eṉṟu collappaṭum akkūṟṟēḻum, īraḷapu icaikkum neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpa - orōvoṉṟu iraṇṭu māttiraiyāka olikkum neṭ ṭeḻuttu eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, aḷavuṅ 6kāraṇakkuṟiyum iṅṅaṉam uṇartti mēl āḷupa. aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ kuṟiya eḻuttiṉ neṭiya vātaṟkuk kuṟ ṟeḻuttākiya iṉan tamakkiṉṟēṉum māttirai yoppumaiyāṉ neṭṭeḻut teṉṟār.
ā ī ū, ē ai eṉpaṉavaṟṟaic coṟcīraṭi yākkuka.	(4)
5.		mūvaḷa picaitta lōreḻut tiṉṟē.
iḵtu - aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; ōreḻuttu mūvaḷapāyum icaik kuṅ kollō veṉṟu aiyappaṭutaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: ōreḻuttu mūvaḷapu icaittaliṉṟu - ōreḻuttē niṉṟu mūṉṟu māttiraiyāka icaittaliṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, pala eḻuttuk kūṭiya iṭattu mūṉṟu māttiraiyum nāṉku māttiraiyum icaikku meṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, perumpāṉmai mūṉṟu māttiraiyē peṟum eṉṟār pulavar. pala eḻutteṉavē, nāṉku māttiraiyum peṟutal peṟṟām.	(5)
6.	nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭaiya
			kūṭṭi yeḻūuta leṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, māttirai nīḷumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ - vaḻakkiṭattuñ ceyyuḷi ṭattum ōcaiyum poruḷum peṟutal kāraṇamāka iraṇṭu māttirai peṟṟa eḻuttu ammāttiraiyiṉ mikku olittalai virumpuvarāyiṉ, avvaḷapu uṭaiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar - tāṅ karutiya māttiraiyait tarutaṟku uriya eḻuttukkaḷaik kūṭṭi ammāttiraikaḷai eḻuppuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
kūṭṭi yeḻuppumāṟu, kuṉṟicaimoḻi (eḻut. 41) ai au veṉṉum (eḻut. 42) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ eḻuvakaitteṉak kūṟupa.
utāraṇam:	āa,  īi,  ūu,  ēe,  aii,  ōo, auu eṉa varum. ivai mūṉṟu māttirai peṟṟaṉa.
7ivaitām neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi (eḻut. 43) eṉṟa anneṭṭeḻuttukkaḷē aḷapeṭuttaliṟ collātal eytiṉa. iṉi aḷapeṭai yacainilai yākalu murittē (cey. 17) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattāṉ eḻuttān taṉmaiyum eytiṟṟu.
itutāṉ, 8iyaṟkaiyaḷapeṭaiyuñ ceyyuṭkup pulavar ceytu koṇṭa 9ceyaṟkai yaḷapeṭaiyumāyc coṟṟaṉmai eyti niṉṟu alaku peṟumāṟuṅ kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ pōla eḻuttāntaṉmai eyti alaku peṟātu niṟkumāṟum ac cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. eṉavē, eḻuttān taṉmaiyum uṭaimai yiṉ aḷapeṭaiyoṭu kūṭi eḻuttu nāṟpatu eṉṟalum porun tiṟṟu. oṟṟaḷapeṭai ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟārāyiṉar.
avvaḷapuṭaiya eṉap paṉmaiyākak kūṟiyavataṉāṉ ivarum nāṉku māttiraiyuṅ koṇṭār; eṉṉai? ivvāciri yarai muntunūl kaṇṭu eṉṟārākaliṉ. māpurāṇattu,
ceyyuṭka ḷōcai citaiyuṅkā līraḷapu
maiyappā ṭiṉṟi yaṇaiyumā maitīroṟṟu
ṟiṉṟiyuñ ceyyuṭ keṭiṉoṟṟai  yuṇṭākku
kuṉṟumē loṟṟaḷapuṅ koḷ.
eṉṟa cūttirattāṉ avar koṇṭa nāṉku māttiraiyum iv vāciriyarkku nērtal vēṇṭutaliṉ. atu ceṟāaay vāḻiya neñcu  (kuṟaḷ. 1200) tūuut tīmpukai tolvicumpu pēeerttatu kol (malaipaṭu.  iṟutiveṇpā) ilāaark killai tamar (nālaṭi. 283) virāaayc ceyyāmai naṉṟu (nālaṭi. 246) marīi ip piṉṉaip pirivu (nālaṭi. 220) eṉac cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭ kellām nāṉku māttirai peṟṟu niṉṟaṉa. aṉṟi mūṉṟu māttirai peṟṟaṉavēl āciriyattaḷai taṭṭuc 10ceppa lōcai keṭumāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam aḷapeṭātu niṉṟu āciriyattaḷai taṭṭu niṟpaṉa kalikku uṟuppākiya koccaka veṇpākkaḷ; ivai aṉṉavaṉṟeṉa uṇarka.
11kōṭṭunūṟum mañcaḷuṅ kūṭiyavaḻip piṟanta cev vaṇṇampōla neṭiluṅ kuṟiluṅ kūṭiya kūṭṭattup piṟanta piṉṉarp piḷavupaṭā vōcaiyai aḷapeṭaiyeṉṟu āciriyar vēṇṭi ṉār. ivai kūṭṭic colliya kālattallatu pulappaṭā, eḷḷāṭṭiyavaḻi yallatu eṇṇey pulappaṭā vāṟu pōla eṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi aḷapeṭaiyallāta ōcaikaḷellām 12icaiyōcai yātaliṉ avaṟṟai aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum (eḻut. 33) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa.	(6)
7.		kaṇṇimai noṭiyeṉa vavvē māttirai
				nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē.
itu, māttiraikku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kaṇṇimai noṭi eṉa avvē māttirai - kaṇṇi maiyeṉavum noṭiyeṉavum avviraṇṭē eḻuttiṉ māttiraikku aḷavu; nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟē - nuṇṇi tāka nūlilakkaṇattai uṇarnta āciriyar kaṇṭa neṟi eṉṟavāṟu.
13eṉa eṇṇiṟ pirintu iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭiṟṟu. kaṇṇimai, noṭi eṉṉum palaporuḷoru coṟkaḷ īṇṭut toḻiṉmēlum ōcaimēlum muṟaiyē niṉṟaṉa. āciriyar ellārum eḻuttiṟku ivaiyē aḷavākak kūṟaliṉ ivaruṅ kūṟiṉār. iyaṟkaimakaṉ taṉkuṟippiṉaṉṟi iraṇṭimai yum orukāṟ kūṭi nīṅkiṉa kālakkaḻivum, a eṉap piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum okkum. ikkaṇṇimai yiṉatu pākam, meykkuñ cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟat tiṟkum itaṉ pākam makarak kuṟukkattiṟkum koḷka. ikkaṇṇimai iraṭṭittu varutal neṭiṟkum atu mūṉṟum nāṉkumāy varutal aḷapeṭaikkuṅ koḷka. atu pōlavē noṭittaṟ ṟoḻiliṟ piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum a eṉap piṟanta ōcaiyatu tōṟṟak kēṭṭuk kālakkaḻivum okkum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkuṅ kūṟiya vāṟē koḷka.
14iṉi avvaḷavaitāṉ niṟuttaḷattal, peytaḷattal, cārttiyaḷattal, nīṭṭiyaḷattal, teṟittaḷattal, tēṅkamukan taḷattal, eṇṇiyaḷattal eṉa eḻuvakaittu. avaṟṟuḷ itu cārttiyaḷattalām.
kaṇṇimaikkum noṭikkum aḷavu ārāyiṉ varampiṉṟi ōṭumeṉṟu karuti nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟu eṉṟu muṭintatu kāṭṭaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ kūṟiṉār. iḵtu āṇai kūṟutalumām. (eṉavē, eḻuttiṟkē aḷavu kūṟi mātti raikku aḷavu kūṟiṟṟilar.) noṭiyiṟ kaṇṇimai ciṟappuṭaittu, uḷḷattāṉ niṉaittu 15nikaḻāmaiyiṉ.	(7)
8.		aukāra viṟuvāyp
				paṉṉī reḻuttu muyireṉa moḻipa.
itu, kuṟilaiyum neṭilaiyun tokuttu vēṟor kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aukāra iṟuvāyp paṉṉīreḻuttum - akaram mutalāka aukāram īṟākak kiṭanta paṉṉiraṇ ṭeḻuttum, uyireṉa moḻipa - uyireṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiyatoru kuṟi. mey patiṉeṭṭiṉaiyum iyakkit tāṉ aruvāy varivaṭiviṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyirāyiṟṟu. ivai meykku uyirāy niṉṟu meykaḷai iyakkumēl uyireṉa vēṟōr eḻuttiṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, meyyiṉiṟkum uyirun taṉiyē niṟkum uyirum vēṟeṉa uṇarka. eṉṉai? akara mutala (kuṟaḷ.1) eṉpuḻi vakaran taṉiyuyirumāyk kakara voṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uyirumāy vēṟu niṟṟaliṉ. av akaran taṉiyē niṟṟalāṉum pala meykkaṇ niṉṟu avvam mey kaṭku icainta ōcaikaḷaip payantē niṟṟalāṉum vēṟupaṭṭa tākaliṉ, oṉṟēyāyum palavēyāyum niṟpator taṉmaiyai yuṭaitteṉṟu kōṭum; iṟaivaṉ oṉṟēyāy niṟkun taṉmaiyum palluyirkkum tāṉēyāy avaṟṟiṉ aḷavāy niṟkun taṉmaiyum pōla. atu a eṉṟavaḻiyum, ūra eṉa viḷiyēṟṟavaḻiyum, akara mutala eṉṟavaḻi mūviṉaṅkaḷil ēṟiṉavaḻiyum, ōcai vēṟu paṭṭavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam icaittuḻiyum māttirai oṉṟēyām. iḵtu ēṉai yuyirkaṭkum okkum.
aukāra iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇputtokai. ummai muṟṟummai. 16akaramutal eṉa muṟkūṟip pōntamaiyiṉ īṇṭu īṟē kūṟiṉār.	(8)
9.		17ṉakāra viṟuvāyp
				patiṉeṇ ṇeḻuttu meyyeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu - uyirallaṉavaṟṟait tokuttu oru kuṟiyīṭu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāra viṟuvāyp patiṉeṇ ṇeḻuttu - kakāram mutal ṉakāram īṟāyk kiṭanta patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttum, meyyeṉa moḻipa - mey yeṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. eṉṉai? paṉṉīruyirkkun tāṉ iṭaṅkoṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkun taṉmai peṟṟa uṭampāy niṟṟaliṉ.
ṉakāraviṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇputtokai. ummai muṟṟummai. 18muṉṉar ṉakāraviṟuvā yeṉpuḻi muppateḻut tiṟkum īṟā meṉṟār, īṇṭup patiṉeṭṭeḻuttiṟkum īṟā meṉṟā rātaliṟ kūṟiyatu kūṟiṟṟaṉṟu.	(9)
10.		meyyō ṭiyaiyiṉu muyiriya ṟiriyā.
iḵtu uyirmeykku aḷapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyir meyyōṭu iyaiyiṉum - paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meyyōṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟaṉavāyiṉum, iyal tiriyā - tam aḷapuṅ kuṟiyum eṇṇun tirintu nillā eṉṟavāṟu.
itu puḷḷi yillā (eḻut. 17) eṉpataṉai nōkki niṟṟaliṉ etiratu pōṟṟalām. uyirum meyyum atikārappaṭuta liṉ īṇṭu vaittār. a eṉpuḻi niṉṟa aḷavuṅ kuṟiyum oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇuṅ, ka eṉa niṉṟa iṭattum okkum. ā eṉpuḻi niṉṟa aḷavuṅ kuṟiyum oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇum, kā eṉa niṉṟa iṭattum okkum eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. piṟavum aṉṉa. āyiṉ oṉṟarai māttiraiyum iraṇṭarai māttiraiyum uṭaiyaṉa oru māttiraiyum iraṇṭu māttiraiyum āyavāṟu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, nīr taṉittu aḷantuḻiyum nāḻiyāy arai nāḻiyuppiṟ kalantuḻiyuṅ kūṭi oṉṟarai nāḻiyāy mikātavāṟu pōlvatōr poruṭpeṟṟi yeṉṟu koḷvatallatu kāraṇaṅ kūṟalākāmai uṇarka. āciriyaṉ āṇai eṉpārum uḷar.
19viḷaṅkāy tiraṭṭiṉā rillaik kaḷaṅkaṉiyai
	kāreṉac ceytāru mil	(nālaṭi.yār 103)
eṉpatē kāṭṭiṉār uraiyāciriyarum.	(10)
11.		meyyi ṉaḷavē yaraiyeṉa moḻipa.
itu, taṉimeykku aḷapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ aḷapē araiyeṉa moḻipa - meyyiṉatu māttiraiyiṉai orōvoṉṟu araimāttirai yuṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
avvarai māttiraiyun taṉittuk kūṟikkāṭṭalākātu, nāc ciṟitu puṭaipeyarun taṉmaiyāy niṟṟaliṉ. iṉi ataṉaic cila moḻimēṟ peytu, kākkai kōṅku kavvai yeṉak kāṭṭupa. 20meyyeṉpatu aḵṟiṇai yiyaṟpeyarātaliṉ meyyeṉṉum oṟṟumai paṟṟi arai yeṉṟār.	(11)
12.		avviya ṉilaiyu mēṉai mūṉṟē.
itu, cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟaṟkum aḷapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉai mūṉṟu - cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟum, av viyal nilaiyum muṟkūṟiya arai māttiraiyākiya iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
kēṇmiyā, nāku,  eḵku eṉa varum.	(12)
13.	araiyaḷavu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭaittē
				yicaiyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kālai.
itu, meykaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: icai iṭaṉ makaram araiyaḷapu kuṟukaluṭaittu - vēṟōr eḻuttiṉatu ōcaiyiṉkaṇ makaravoṟṟut taṉ arai māttiraiyiṟ kuṟukik kālmāttirai peṟutalaiyuṭaittu, teriyuṅkālai arukum - ārāyuṅ kālattut tāṉ ciṟupāṉmai yāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: pōṉm,  varumvaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa oru moḻik kaṇṇum irumoḻikkaṇṇuṅ koḷka. 21itu piṟaṉ kōṭ kūṟaleṉṉum utti. (13)

14.		22uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.
itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭai varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai cey kiṉṟatu.
makaram atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār.
itaṉ poruḷ: uṭpeṟupuḷḷi - puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟ peṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum - makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē puṟattup peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ meykaṭkuk kūṟum puḷḷi. īṇṭu 23uru eṉṟatu kāṭcip poruḷai uṇarttiniṉṟatu.
utāraṇam: kappi (kammi) eṉavarum.
iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal.	(14)
15.		meyyi ṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.
itu, taṉimeykkum uyirmeykkum oppumaimēl vēṟṟumai ceytal kūṟukiṉṟatu; eṉṉai? uyirmeyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkun taṉi meyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkum vaṭivu oṉṟāka eḻutiṉavaṟṟai oṟṟākkuvataṟkup piṉpu puḷḷi peṟuka veṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ iyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal - patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷiṉ taṉmaiyāṉatu puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟṟalām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, uyirmeykaṭkup puḷḷiyiṉṟāyiṟṟu. k ṅ ...... ṟ ṉ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟaip puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭavē, puḷḷi peṟuvataṟku muṉṉar akaram uṭaṉiṉṟatōr meyvaṭivē peṟṟu niṉṟaṉa vaṟṟai, piṉṉar appuḷḷiyiṭṭut taṉimey yākkiṉāreṉpa tūum peṟutum. itaṉāṉē kakaram ṅakaram mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟuvataṟku muṉṉar iyalpāka akaram peṟṟē niṟkum eṉpa tūum,  puḷḷi peṟuṅkālattu avvakaram nīṅkumeṉpatūum, piṉṉar appuḷḷi nīṅki uyirēṟumiṭattut taṉkaṇ 24akaram nīṅkiyēpōka, varukiṉṟatōr uyir yātāyiṉum oṉṟu ēṟi niṟkumeṉpatūum peṟṟām. meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum (eḻut. 46) eṉṉuñ cūttirat tāṉum ituvē itaṟkuk karuttātal uṇarka.(15)
16.		ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyu maṟṟē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ekara okarattu iyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē - ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉatu nilaiyum meypōlap puḷḷipeṟum iyalpiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭkup puḷḷi yiṉṟāyiṟṟu.
e- o - eṉa varum.
iḵtu uyirmeykkum okkum.
makaram ārāyccippaṭṭatu kaṇṭu, makarattiṟku vaṭivu vēṟṟumai ceytu, atikārattāṉ meyyiṉ taṉmai kūṟi, ataṉ piṉ 25māṭṭēṟṟaliṉ ekara okarattaiyuṅ kūṟiṉār.	(16)
17.		puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyu
					muruvuru vāki yakaramō ṭuyirttalu
					mēṉai yuyirō ṭuruvutirin tuyirttalu
					māyī riyala vuyirtta lāṟē.
itu, meyyum uyiruṅ kūṭip puṇarumāṟum āṇṭu avai tiriyātun tirintum niṟkumāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷi illā ellā meyyum - uyiraip peṟu taṟkup puḷḷiyaip pōkkiṉa ellā meykaḷum, uru uruvāki akaramōṭu uyirttalum - puḷḷi peṟukiṉṟa kālattu iyal pākiya akaram nīṅkiya vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivāki niṉṟu piṉṉar ēṟiya akarattoṭu kūṭi olittalum, ēṉai uyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttalum - oḻinta patiṉōruyiruṅ kūṭi avvaṭivu tirintu olittalum, āyīriyala uyirttalāṟē - eṉa av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya, avai olikkum muṟaimai eṉṟavāṟu.
puḷḷiyillā mey yeṉavē, muṉ peṟṟuniṉṟa puḷḷiyai uyirēṟṟutaṟkup pōkkiṉamai peṟutum. uruvuruvāki yeṉavē puḷḷi peṟutaṟkāka iyalpākiya akaram nīṅkiya vaṭivē piṉṉar akaram peṟutaṟku vaṭivāmeṉpatu kūṟiṉār.
ka - ṅa - ya eṉa varum.
uruvu tirintu uyirttalāvatu mēluṅ kīḻum vilaṅku peṟṟum, kōṭu peṟṟum, 26puḷḷi peṟṟum, puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟum uyirttalām. ki kī mutaliyaṉa mēlvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ku kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻ vilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke kē mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. kā ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa.  arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyai ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyai vaḷaitteḻutiṉār. ko ko ṅo ṅo mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyuṅ kōṭum uṭaṉ peṟṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉan tirintu olippavē uyirmey paṉṉiru patiṉeṭṭu irunūṟ ṟorupattāṟāyiṟṟu. ākavē uyirmeykku vaṭivum oruvāṟṟāṟ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē, mey taṉakku iyalpākiya akarattai nīṅki niṟpatoru taṉmaiyum, piṟitōruyirai ēṟkun taṉmaiyum uṭaiya teṉpatūum, uyir, meykkaṇ pulappaṭātu iyalpākiya akaramāy niṟkun taṉmaiyum mey puḷḷipeṟ ṟaḻintavaḻi avaṟṟiṟkut takka uyirāyp pulappaṭṭu varun taṉmaiyum uṭaiya teṉpatūum peṟṟām. uyirmey eṉpatu ummait tokai.	(17)
18.	meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē.
itu meyyum uyiruṅ kūṭiyavaḻi avaṟṟiṉ ōcai niṟkum muṟaimai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  meyyiṉ 27vaḻiyatu - meyyiṉatu ōcai tōṉṟiya piṉṉatām, uyir tōṉṟu nilaiyē - uyiriṉatu ōcai tōṉṟu nilai eṉṟavāṟu.
muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ mey muṉṉar niṟpa uyir piṉ vantu ēṟumeṉṟār. ammuṟaiyē ōcaiyum piṟakkumeṉṟār. itaṉāṉē māttirai koḷḷuṅkāl uppum nīrum pōla oṉṟē yāy niṟṟalum, vēṟupaṭuttuk kāṇuṅkāl viralum viraluñ cēra niṉṟāṟ pōla vēṟāy niṟṟalum peṟṟām. nīr uppiṉ kuṇamē yāyavāṟu pōla, uyirum meyyiṉ kuṇamēyāy vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai eyti niṟṟal koḷka.
utāraṇam: ka - ṅa - ya eṉak kūṭṭamum pirivum mūvakaiyōcaiyuṅ kāṇka.	(18)
19.	valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.
itu, taṉimeykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟor kuṟiyīṭu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa - ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉun taṉi meykaḷai, valleḻutteṉpa - valleḻutteṉṉuṅ 28kuṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
iḵtu āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. oḻinta melleḻuttaiyum iṭaiyeḻuttaiyum nōkkat tām valleṉṟicaittalāṉum, valleṉat talaivaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttāyiṉa.	(19)
20.	melleḻut teṉpa ṅañaṇa namaṉa.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉun taṉi meykaḷai, melleḻutteṉpa - melleḻut teṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. melleṉṟi caittalāṉum, melleṉṟa mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṟ piṟattalāṉum melleḻuttāyiṟṟu.	(20)
21.	iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉun taṉi meykaḷai, iṭaiyeḻutteṉpa - iṭaiyeḻutteṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. iṭai nikara vāy olittalāṉum, iṭainikarttāya miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṟ piṟatta lāṉum iṭai yeḻuttāyiṟṟu.
valliṉattuk ka - ca - ta - pa eṉṉum nāṉkum, melliṉattu  ña - na - ma eṉṉum mūṉṟum, iṭaiyiṉattu ya - va eṉṉum iraṇṭum moḻikku mutalātal nōkki immuṟaiyē vaittār. 29ippeyarāṉē eḻutteṉṉum ōcaikaḷ uruvāyiṉa. uyirkkuṅ kuṟumai neṭumai kūṟaliṉ avaiyum uruvāyiṉa. itu cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟat tiṟkum okkum.	(21)
22.		30ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
						meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉilai teriyuṅ kālai.
itu, taṉimey, piṟameyyōṭun taṉ meyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum, uyirmey - uyirmeyyōṭun taṉimey yōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ammūvāṟum - aṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu kūṟākap pakutta patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ - vaḻakkiṭattuñ ceyyuḷiṭattum eḻuttukkaḷaik kūṭṭi moḻippaṭuttu vaḻaṅkutal uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅkum nilai - taṉi mey taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa piṟa meyyōṭun taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyum, uṭaṉ mayaṅkum nilai - ap patiṉeṭṭum uyiruṭaṉē niṉṟu taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭun taṉi meyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyumeṉa iraṇṭām; teriyuṅkālai - avai mayaṅkum moḻiyān taṉmai ārāyuṅ kālattu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, taṉittu niṉṟa eḻuttuṭaṉ muṉṉiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ tāṅ kūṭumāṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. kaṭka eṉṟāl iṭai niṉṟa ṭakara mey muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉiṉ vēṟāya kakara voṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kākkai eṉṟāl, iṭainiṉṟa kakaravoṟṟu muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. karu eṉa īreḻuttoru moḻiyuṅ karutu eṉa mūveḻuttoru moḻiyum uyirmey niṉṟu tam muṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmey yōṭu mayaṅkiṉa. tuṇaṅkai eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kal vil eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu.
teriyuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyir muṉṉar uyirmeym mayakkamum uyir muṉṉart taṉimeym mayakkamum koḷka. avai aḷai, āmpal eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
im mayakkaṅkaḷuḷ taṉimeymuṉṉarp piṟamey niṉṟu mayaṅkutal palavātaliṟ pala cūttirattāṟ kūṟit taṉ muṉṉart tāṉ vantu mayaṅkutalai oru cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa. avai mayaṅkuṅkāl valliṉattil ṭakaramum ṟakaramum, melliṉamāṟum, iṭaiyiṉa māṟum piṟa meyyōṭu mayaṅkumeṉṟum, valliṉattiṟ kacatapakkaḷ tam meyyōṭaṉṟip piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkā veṉṟum uyttuṇarak kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.
mūvāṟu meṉṉum ummai  - muṟṟummai.
iccūttiram mutalāka meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ (eḻut. 30) 31īṟāka mēṟkūṟum moḻimarapiṟkup poruntiya karuvi kūṟukiṉṟa teṉṟuṇarka; eḻuttukkaḷ tammiṟ kūṭip puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatātaliṉ.	(22)
23.		ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
					ka ca pa veṉṉu mūveḻut turiya.
itu taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkum mayakkam uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - moḻiyiṭai niṉṟa ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum nāṉku puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca pa eṉṉum mūveḻuttu uriya - ka ca pa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	kaṭka kaṭci kaṭpa eṉavuṅ, kaṟka muyaṟci kaṟpa eṉavuñ, celka valci celpa eṉavuṅ, koḷka nīḷciṉai koḷpa eṉavuṟ taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkiya vāṟu kāṇka. kaṭciṟār kaṟciṟār eṉpaṉa irumoḻip puṇarcci yākaliṉ īṇṭaikkākā.	(23)
24.		avaṟṟuḷ
					laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar - lakāra ḷakāramākiya puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yavavun tōṉṟum - kacapakkaḷē yaṉṟi yakara vakaraṅkaḷum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
kolyāṉai, celvam, veḷyāṟu, kaḷvaṉ eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuṭ kolyāṉai eṉa viṉaittokaiyum, veḷ yāṟu eṉap paṇputtokaiyum nilaimoḻi varumoḻi ceyvataṟku iyaiyāmaiyiṉ 32maruviṉ pāttiya (eḻut. 482) eṉṟu kūṟuvārātaliṉ ivvāciriyar ivaṟṟai orumoḻi yākak koḷvareṉṟu uṇarka. ikkaruttāṉē mēlum viṉait tokaiyum paṇputtokaiyum oru moḻiyākak koṇṭu utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭutum. aṉṟi ivvāciriyar nūl ceykiṉṟa kālattu viṉaittokaik kaṇṇum paṇputtokaikkaṇṇu maṉṟi oru moḻikkaṇṇē mayaṅkuvaṉavum uḷavātaliṉ, avaṟṟaik kaṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉār; avai piṉṉar iṟantaṉa veṉṟu oḻittu utāraṇamillaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭāmaṟ pōtalē naṉṟeṉṟu kūṟalumoṉṟu.	(24)
25.		ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉart
					tatta micaika ḷottaṉa nilaiyē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, tattam micaikaḷ - tamakkiṉamāy muṉṉiṉṟa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa kkaḷ, ottaṉa nilaiyē - piṉṉiṟṟaṟkup poruntiṉa mayaṅki niṟṟaṟkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu..
utāraṇam: kaṅkaṉ, kañcaṉ, kaṇṭaṉ, kantaṉ, kampaṉ, maṉṟaṉ eṉa varum. teṅku, piñcu, vaṇṭu, pantu, kampu, kaṉṟu eṉak kuṟṟukaramuṅ kāṭṭupa.	(25)
26.		avaṟṟuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
					ka ca ña pa ma ya vav vēḻu muriya.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟiya melloṟṟu āṟaṉuḷ, ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar - ṇakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar,  ka ca ña pa ma  ya va ēḻum uriya - ṭaṟakkaḷē yaṉṟik ka ca ña pa ma ya va eṉṉum ēḻeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 33eṇku veṇcāntu veṇñāṇ paṇpu veṇmai maṇyāṟu eṇvaṭṭu eṉavum, puṉku puṉcey meṉ ñāṇ aṉpu vaṉmai iṉyāḻ puṉvaraku eṉavum varum. eṇvaṭṭu - viṉaittokai. eṇku, puṉku - peyar.	(26)
27.		ña na ma va veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
					yaḵkā ṉiṟṟaṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ:ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - ña na ma va eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal mey peṟṟaṉṟē - yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal poruṇmai peṟṟatu eṉṟavāṟu..
iṅṅaṉam āciriyar cūttirañ ceytaliṉ, akkālattu oru moḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ uḷaveṉpatu peṟṟām. avai ikkālattu iṟantaṉa.
iṉi, 34uraiyāciriyar uriñyātu porunyātu tirumyātu tevyātu eṉa irumoḻikkaṇ varuvaṉa utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārā leṉiṉ, āciriyar orumoḻi yāmāṟu īṇṭuk kūṟi, irumoḻi puṇarttaṟkup puṇariyaleṉṟu vēṟōr iyaluṅkūṟi, ataṉkaṇ meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum (eḻut. 107) eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. kūṟip piṉṉum ukara moṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti (eḻut. 163) eṉṟum, piṟāṇṭum īṟukaṭōṟum eṭuttōtip puṇarppar. ātaliṉ īṇṭu iru moḻip puṇarcci kāṭṭiṟ kūṟiyatu kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamām. ataṉāl avai kāṭṭutal poruntāmai uṇarka.	(27)
28.		maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ - muṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ makara mākiya puḷḷi muṉṉar, vavvun tōṉṟum - pakara yakaramē yaṉṟi vakaramum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṟkum utāraṇam ikkālattu iṟantatu. aṉṟi, varum vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kāṭṭiṉ vakāra micaiyu makāraṅ kuṟukum (eḻut. 330) eṉṟa viti vēṇṭāvām.	(28)
29.		yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
					mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar - yaraḻa eṉṟu kūṟap paṭum mūṉṟu puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, mutalā keḻuttum - moḻikku mutalāmeṉa mēṟkūṟum oṉpateḻuttukkaḷum, ummaiyāṉ moḻikku mutalākāta piṟa eḻuttukkaḷum, ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum - ṅakāramum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	āyka ārka āḻka, āytal ārtal āḻtal, āynar ārnar āḻnar, āypavai ārpavai āḻpavai, vāymai nērmai kīḻmai, eycilai vārcilai vāḻcēri, teyvam cērvatu vāḻvatu, pāyñekiḻi nērñekiḻi vāḻñeṇṭu, ceyyāṟu pōryāṉai vīḻyāṉai eṉa moḻikku mutalām oṉpatum vantu mayaṅkiṉa. ceyyāṟu eṉa yakarattiṉ muṉṉar yakaram vantatu taṉmuṉṉart tāṉ vantatām.
iṉi, ummaiyāṟkoṇṭa moḻikku mutalākātavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇuñ cila kāṭṭutum: 35ōyvu cōrvu vāḻvu, ōyvōr cōrvōr vāḻvōr, āyñar cērñar āḻñar eṉa varum. piṟa eḻuttukkaḷōṭu varuvaṉa uḷavēṉum vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum nōkkik kūṟikkoḷka.
iṅku, vēyṅṅaṉam vērṅṅaṉam vīḻṅṅaṉam eṉa  moḻikku mutalākāta ṅakaram iṭaivanta coṟkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟuṇarka, āciriyar ōtutaliṉ. itaṉai ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum eṉap pirittōtiṉār, akkālattum aritāka vaḻaṅkaliṉ.
iṉi vēykaṭitu vērkaṭitu vīḻkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avai iru moḻiyāka nilaimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu mēṟpuṇarkkiṉṟaṉa īṇṭaikkākā eṉa maṟukka.	(29)
30.		meynnilai cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttun
					tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
itu, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē taṉimey taṉṉoṟṟoṭu, mayaṅkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ - poruṇilai maiyaik karutiṉ, ellā eḻuttum - patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, tammuṉ tām varūum - tam muṉṉē tām vantu mayaṅkum, raḻa alaṅkaṭaiyē - rakāra ḻakāraṅka ḷallāta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	 kākkai, eṅṅaṉam, paccai, maññai, paṭṭai, maṇṇai, tattai, venney, appu, ammai, veyyar, elli, evvi, kaḷḷi, koṟṟi, kaṉṉi eṉa varum.
meynnilai cuṭṭiṉeṉṟataṉāl taṉimey muṉṉar uyir mey varumeṉṟu koḷka. ellāmeṉṟatu rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ oḻintaṉavaṟṟait taḻuviṟṟu.	(30)
31.		a i u am mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.
itu, kuṟṟeḻut teṉṟavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: a i u am mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu - a i u eṉṟu kūṟiya ammūṉṟuñ cuṭṭeṉṉum kuṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi, cuṭṭi aṟiyappaṭum poruḷai uṇarttaliṉ. taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ ekaram viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttaluṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam:	akkoṟṟaṉ, ikkoṟṟaṉ, ukkoṟṟaṉ, epporuḷ eṉa varum. ivai peyaraic cārntu tattaṅ kuṟippiṟ poruḷ ceyta iṭaiccol. iccūttiram 36orutalaimoḻital eṉṉum utti. ituvum mēlaic cūttiramum eḻuttān taṉmaiyaṉṟi moḻi nilaimaippaṭṭu vēṟoru kuṟipeṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ moḻi marapiṉaic cēra vaittār.	(31)
32.		ā ē ō am mūṉṟum viṉā.
itu, neṭṭeḻutteṉṟavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉā ā ē ō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ammūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyiṉai yuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu.
ituvum āṭciyuṅ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, viṉāp poruḷ uṇarttaliṉ.
utāraṇam: 37uṇkā, uṇkē, uṇkō eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ 38ākāram ikkālattu viṉāvāy varutalaritu. nīyē nīyō eṉpaṉa ikkālattu varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ē ō eṉpaṉa iṭaiccol lōttiṉuḷḷuṅ kūṟiṉār, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ tarum poruṭṭokaipaṟṟi. 39itu moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻi yātataṉai muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal eṉṉum uttikku iṉamām,  yakara ākāramum viṉāvāy   varutaliṉ. 	(32)
33.		aḷapiṟan tuyirttalu moṟṟicai nīṭalu
					muḷaveṉa moḻipa vicaiyoṭu civaṇiya
					narampiṉ maṟaiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟaleṉṉum utti paṟṟi icainūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇamāmāṟukūṟi, avvilakkaṇam innūṟkuṅ koḷkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḷapiṟantu uyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - muṟkūṟiya uyirum uyirmeyyum māttiraiyai iṟantolittalum, oṟṟeḻut tukkaḷ araimāttiraiyiṉ nīṇṭolittalum, yāḻ nūliṭattaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; icaiyoṭu civaṇiya uḷaveṉa moḻipa - aṅṅaṉam aḷapiṟantum nīṇṭum icaittal ōcaiyōṭu poruntiya nālvakaic ceyyuḷ kaḷukkum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
eḻuttukaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiraiyai 40iṟantolikku māṟu kaṇṭu, avai iṟantolikkum iṭaṅkūṟiṉār, eḻuttuñ collum poruḷuṅ kiṭakkum iṭañ ceyyuḷiṭamātaliṉ. atu mikku olittalaic ceyyuḷiyaliṉkaṇ māttirai yeḻuttiya lacaivakai yeṉāa (cey. 1) eṉa irupattāṟu uṟuppiṟkuñ ciṟappuṟuppāka muṟkūṟip piṉṉar,
māttirai yaḷavu meḻuttiyal vakaiyu
mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar	(cey-2)
eṉa iccūttirattoṭu māṭṭeṟintu, piṉṉum,
eḻuttaḷa veñciṉuñ cīrnilai tāṉē
kuṉṟalu mikutalu milleṉa moḻipa	(cey-43)
eṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār. 41itu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟu.
utāraṇam:	varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay valippaṉyāṉ kēeḷiṉi (kali. 11) eṉpuḻi ḻakara ākāramum kakara ēkāramum māttirai iṟantolittavāṟu uṇarka. piṭiyūṭṭip piṉṉuṇṇuṅṅ kaḷiṟeṉavum uraittaṉarē (kali . 11) eṉpuḻi ṅakaravoṟṟu aḷaviṟantavāṟu kāṇka. oḻinta mūvakaic ceyyuḷkaṭkum ivvāṟē tattamakkuriya pā eṉṉum uṟuppiṉai naṭātti aḷavu mikumāṟu kāṇka.
civaṇiya eṉpatu 42toḻiṟpeyar. icaiyoṭu civaṇiya eṉavē ceyyuḷātal peṟṟām. narampu eṉṟatu ākupeyarāy yāḻiṉai uṇarttiṟṟu. maṟaiyeṉṟatu nūlai.
moḻipa veṉṟum, eṉmaṉār pulavareṉṟum irukāṟ kūṟiyavataṉāl, iṅṅaṉam poruḷ kūṟalē āciriyarkkuk karuttā yiṟṟu; eṉṉai? ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kūṟiya māttirai yaḷavum (cey.2) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉ mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa (cey.2) eṉṉum māṭṭēṟṟiṟku *ivvōttiṉuḷ vēṟōr cūttiram iṉmaiyiṉ. ivvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟākkāṟ ceyyuṭkup pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻātu, avai uraicceyyuṭ pōla niṟṟaliṉ iv vilakkaṇaṅ kūṟavē vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarka.
cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
viṉṟi yamaiyā tiyaipavai yellā
moṉṟa vuraippa turaiyeṉap paṭumē	(marapiyal. 103)
eṉṉum marapiyaṟ cūttirattāṉē ivvāṟē cūttiraṅkaḷai nalintu poruḷuraippaṉa vellāṅ koḷka.	(33)
nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	eṭṭu vakaiyāvaṉa eṉavum pāṭam appāṭamē nalam. eṉṉai? muṉ eṭṭuvakaiyāṉum eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarē vakuttuk kūṟi yiruttaliṉ.
2.	eṭṭiṟanta pala vakaiyāvaṉa eṉavum pāṭam. ap pāṭamē nalam. eṉṉai? muṉ eṭṭiṟanta palavakaiyāṉum eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarē vakuttuk kūṟiyiruttaliṉ. 
3.	iraṇṭum eṉṟatu kuṟṟiyalukara kuṟṟiyalikaraṅkaḷai. avai ummai tokkuniṉṟaṉa eṉṟatu kuṟṟiyalukaramum kuṟṟiyalikaramum eṉa ummai peṟṟu niṟkavēṇṭiyaṉa avvummaikaḷ tokkuk kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉa niṉṟaṉa eṉṟapaṭi. kuṟṟiyalukaram uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭuttu niṟṟalum muṟṟiyalukaram uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭāmaiyum avviraṇṭaṟku muḷḷa vēṟupāṭeṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. itaṉāl kuṟṟiyalukarattukku muṉṉum muṟṟiyalukarattukku muṉṉum uyirmutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl avai aṭaiyum puṇarcci vēṟṟumai aṟiyappaṭum. ēṉaik kaṇam varuṅkālum avai aṭaiyum puṇarcci vēṟṟumaikaḷai aṟintu koḷka. varakiyātu eṉpuḻi varaku eṉpataṉ  muṉ yakara mutaṉmoḻi vantaviṭattu varaku eṉpatiluḷḷa ukaraṅ keṭṭu ikaram virintu kuṟuki niṉṟatākaliṉ avvikaram kuṟṟiyalikaram eṉappaṭumaṉṟi muṟṟiya likaram eṉappaṭātu. ataṉāl iyalpāṉa ikaravīṟṟup puṇarccikkum kuṟṟiyalikarap puṇarccikkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭaṟiyappaṭum. 
	kātu eṉṉuñ collai itaḻ kuvittuc collumiṭattu muṟṟiyalu karamām. ataṟkup poruḷ kol eṉpatu. ataṉai itaḻ kuviyāmaṟ collumiṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaramām. ataṟkup poruḷ kātu eṉṉum uṟuppu. murukku eṉṉuñ col itaḻ kuvittuc collumiṭattu aḻi eṉavum. itaḻkuviyāmaṟ collumiṭattu murukkākiya marameṉavum poruḷ tarum. piṟavum ivvāṟē poruḷ vēṟṟumai uṭaiyavātal aṟintu koḷka. kuṟṟiyalukarattukkum muṟṟiyalukarattukkumuḷḷa poruḷ vēṟṟumai ivaiyē. araciyātu eṉpuḻi araci eṉpati luḷḷa ikarattai muṟṟiya vikaramākak koḷḷiṉ ataṉai ōr ikaravīṟṟu uyartiṇaip peyarākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷuṅkāl atu yātu eṉpataṉōṭu iyaiyātu. ātaliṉ aracu eṉpatē collum poruḷum eṉpatum atiluḷḷa ikaraṅ kuṟṟiyalikara meṉpatum peṟappaṭum. paṭavē araci eṉpatiluḷḷa ikarattai iyalpākak koḷḷuṅkāl itu poruḷākumeṉavum kuṟṟiyalikaramākak koḷḷuṅkāl itu poruḷāku meṉavum avviraṇṭaṟkumuḷḷa poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyaṟiyappaṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṉṉum "caṅkan tarumutti yāmpeṟa" eṉṉun tirukkōvaiyārc ceyyuḷaṭikkuc cilēṭaip poruḷ koḷḷumpoḻutu muttu eṉṟum mutti eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum. koḷḷuṅkāl mutti eṉpatiluḷḷa ikarattaik kuṟṟiyalikaramākak koṇṭu muttu eṉṟum, muṟṟiyalikaramākak koṇṭu mutti eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutaliṉālē kuṟṟiyalikaram muṟṟiyalikaram eṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷa poruḷ vēṟṟumai naṉkaṟiyappaṭutal kāṇka.
	kātu - nērpu acai. varaku - niraipuacai. naṭuvuvāṅki yiṭṭeḻutal ḵ ippaṭip puḷḷiyiṭāmal ḵ ippaṭi uḷvaḷaittiṭṭeḻutal. naṭuvu - uḷ. vāṅkal - vaḷaittal. eḻuttiyal taḻāaōcai - kaṭaloli caṅkoli pōlvaṉa. muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirameṉṟatu mutalāñ cūttirattai.    
	iccūttirattukku ivvāṟu poruḷkoḷḷātu pērāciriyar kuṟṟiya lukaramum kuṟṟiyalikaramum āytamumeṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷi yeḻuttuk kaḷum eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar. ataṉai, vārātataṉāl vantatu muṭittal eṉṉum utti uraiyuḷ iccūttirattai eṭuttukkāṭṭi immūṉṟum puḷḷiyeḻuttukkaḷeṉṟu avar kūṟutalāṉaṟintu koḷka. ivarait taḻuvic civañāṉa muṉivarum tol - cūttira viruttiyuḷ ivvāṟu kūṟuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum kuṟṟiyalikaramum akkālattup puḷḷi peṟṟu vaḻaṅkiyateṉpatu "kuṟṟiya likaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaram maṟṟavai tāmē puḷḷi peṟumē" eṉṉum caṅka yāppuc cūttirattāṉu maṟiyappaṭum. (yāp - virutti 27m pakkam). 
4.	iraṇṭu māttirai peṟṟa eḻuttai nōkki oru māttirai peṟṟa eḻuttuk kuṟṟeḻut teṉappaṭṭāṟpōla, oru māttirai peṟṟa kuṟ ṟeḻuttai nōkki araimāttirai peṟṟa meyyeḻuttum kuṟṟe ḻutteṉappaṭalāmēyeṉiṉ? tamakkiṉamāyavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇaṉṟē kuṟumai neṭumai koḷvatu; akkuṟṟeḻuttuk tamak kiṉamallā maiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭātāyiṟṟu. aṉṟiyum oru māttirai peṟṟa meykaḷ tamakkiṉamāka iruppiṉum arai māttirai peṟṟa meykaḷākiya tām kuṟṟeḻutteṉappaṭalām. atuvu miṉmaiyiṉ kuṟṟeḻutteṉap paṭāvāyiṉa eṉṟapaṭi.
5.	icaippatu - eḻuttu. icai - oli. atu poruṭṭaṉmaiyeṉṟatu anta icai poruḷiṉ kuṇameṉṟapaṭi. iṅkē poruḷ eḻuttu. ataṉ kuṇam oli eṉpatām. eṉavē eḻuttu vēṟu icai vēṟu eṉṟu uṇaraṟka eṉṟapaṭi. ēṉ avvāṟu kūṟiṉāreṉiṉ ōraḷapicaikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻutteṉa icaiyai vēṟākavum eḻuttai vēṟākavum āciriyar kūṟiyataṉāl eṉka.   
6.	kāraṇakkuṟi - kāraṇappeyar; eṉṟatu kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṉum peyarai. kuṟiya icaiyaiyuṭaiyatākiya eḻuttu; neṭiya icaiyaiyuṭaiyatākiya eḻuttu eṉpatu karuttu. kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭe ḻuttu eṉpaṉa paṇputtokai. mēlāḷupa eṉṟatu neṭṭeḻutteṉa mēlum eṭuttāḷutalai.
7.	ā a eṉpuḻi ā eṉṉum ōreḻut torumoḻiyākiya neṭṭe ḻuttē aḷapeṭuttaliṉ avvaḷapeṭai yeḻuttum anneṭṭe ḻuttōṭu cērntu moḻiyāmeṉpār. ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya neṭṭeḻuttē aḷapeṭuttaliṟ collātal eytiṟṟeṉṟār. eṉavē aḷapeṭai eḻuttukkup poruḷillai yāyiṉum ataṟku muṉ ṉuḷḷa neṭṭeḻuttukkup poruḷuṇmaiyiṉ ataṉ poruḷē taṉakkum poruḷāyc collān taṉmai eyti niṟkumeṉṟapaṭi. collān taṉmai yeyti niṟṟalāvatu; tāṉu mammoḻik kuṟuppe ḻuttuppōla niṉṟu acaiyāki alakupeṟutal. eḻuttān taṉmai yeytalāvatu aṅṅaṉañ coṟkuṟuppuppōla niṉṟa aḷapeṭai yeḻuttu. tamakku muṉṉuḷḷa neṭṭeḻuttē alakupeṟat tām alaku peṟātu (m~jhtJ ā a eṉpuḻi aḷaṭaiyeḻuttākiya akaram taṉakku muṉṉuḷḷa neṭṭeḻuttōṭu cērntu alakupeṟṟu nērnēr eṉa nillāmal an neṭṭeḻuttē taṉittu alakupeṟṟu nēr eṉa niṟpat tāṉ alaku bgwhJ) aḷapeṭai yeḻutteṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭu niṟṟal. eṉavē atu aḷapeṭai eḻutteṉṟu nīkkappaṭuvataṉṟi acaikkuriya eḻuttāka vaittu eṇṇappaṭātu eṉpatu karuttu. aḷapeṭai yacainilaiyākalāvatu aḷapeṭaiyeḻuttu, tāṉumōracaiyākic cīrnilai yaṭaiyātu. tamakku muṉṉuḷḷa neṭ ṭeḻuttōṭu cērntu ōracaiyākavē koḷḷappaṭṭu niṟṟal.
8.	iyaṟkai yaḷapeṭai kurīi, tōḻīi eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa. ivai vaḻakkiṟkuñ ceyyuṭkum potuvākaliṉ iyaṟkaiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. tōḻīi eṉpatait toḻīi eṉṟukoḷvāru muḷar. (kali - 103-m ceyyuṭ kuṟippup pārkka.)
9.	ceyaṟkai yaḷapeṭai pulavaṉ ceyyuḷōcai niṟaittaṟ poruṭṭut tāṉē ceytukoṇṭa aḷapeṭai. atu ōotalvēṇṭum eṉpatu pōlvaṉa. coṟṟaṉmai yeyti niṟṟalum eḻuttān taṉmai yeytiniṟṟalum muṉ viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. alakupeṟutal - acaikkuriya eḻuttāka eṇṇappeṟutal. iyaṟkaiyaḷapeṭai ceyaṟkaiyaḷa peṭaikaḷiṉ iyalpaic ceyyuḷiyal 17-m cūttiram nōkki uṇarka.
10.	ceppalōcai - veṇpāvukkuriya ōcai. aḷapeṭaikku nāṉku māttiraiyuṅ koḷḷākkāl āṉṟōriyaṟṟiya veṇpākkaḷil nāṉku māttirai peṟuvaṉa āciriyattaḷai taṭṭuc ceppalōcai keṭum. ātalāl nāṉku māttiraiyuṅ koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉpatām. kalikkuṟuppākiya koccaka veṇpākkaḷ āciriyattaḷai taṭṭum niṟkalām. `ceṟāaay vāḻiya neñcu' mutaliya avvāṟu niṟṟal kūṭā eṉka.
11.	kōṭṭunūṟu - caṅkunūṟu - cuṇṇāmpu.
12.	icai ōcai eṉṟatu - pāṭṭiṉ icaiyai niṟaikkum ōcaiyai eṉpatu īṇṭu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. ēṉeṉil "aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum" eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ kūṟupa eṉṟu kūṟaliṉ.  
13.	eṉa eṉṉum iṭaiccol eṇṇup poruṭkaṇ varutaliṉ atu kaṇṇimaiyōṭuṅ kūṭṭappaṭṭu kaṇṇimaiyeṉa noṭiyeṉa niṟkumeṉpār eṉa eṇṇiṟ pirintu iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭiṟṟeṉṟār. kaṇṇimai toḻiṉmēlum noṭi ōcai mēlum niṉṟaṉa eṉka. iyaṟkai makaṉ eṉṟatu imaittaṟṟoḻilil vikāramillāta makaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. kuṟippu - niṉaivu. pākam - caripaṅku.
14.	niṟuttaḷattal - tulā mutaliya niṟaiyaḷavaikaḷāl niṟuttaḷattal. pey taḷattal - oṉṟiṉuṭ peytaḷattal eṉṟatu kalamutaliyaṟṟiṟ peytaḷat talai. cārttiyaḷattal - oṉṟaṉaḷavōṭu maṟṟoṉ ṟaṉaḷavai oppiṭṭu nōkkiyaḷattal. teṟittaḷattal - oṉṟaṉaip puṭaittu oliyai yuṇṭākki ataṉaic ceviyāṟ kēṭṭu nitāṉittu aḷantukōṭal. atu mattaḷam vīṇai mutaliyavaṟṟaip puṭaittu avaṟṟoliyaic cevi karuviyāka aḷantu kōṭal. tēṅkamukan taḷattal - nāḻi mutaliya vaṟṟāṉaḷattal.
15.	nikaḻttāmaiyiṉ eṉṟiruppatu nalam. 
16.	`akaramutal' eṉa muṟkūṟip pōntamaiyiṉ eṉṟatu mutaṟ cūttirattil  `akaramutal' eṉak kūṟiyamaiyai. eṉavē, āṇṭuk kūṟiya ataṉai īṇṭuk kūṟiya `aukāra viṟuvāy' eṉpataṉōṭu cērttu `akaramutal aukāraviṟuvāyp paṉṉīreḻuttu muyireṉa moḻipa' eṉak kūṟuka eṉṟapaṭi.
17.	itaṉkaṇ "kakaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy" eṉak kūṟappaṭa villaiyē yeṉiṉ? atu mutaṟcūttirattu muppatu eṉṟataṉālum itaṉkaṇ `patiṉeṇ' eḻuttum eṉṟataṉālum piṉvarum "valleḻutteṉpa         ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa" eṉṉuñ cūttiram mutaliyavaṟṟāṉum uyttuṇarntu koḷḷappaṭum. iccūttiramum mutaṟcūttiramum neṭuṅkaṇakkai anuvatittuk kūṟiyaṉavākum.
18.	muṉṉar eṉṟatu 1-m cūttirattai.
19.	viḷaṅkāykkut tiraṭciyum. kaḷaṅkaṉikkuk karuniṟamum iyaṟkaiyātal pōla. uyirmeyk kuṟilkaḷ orumāttirai peṟutalum, uyirmey neṭilkaḷ iraṇṭumāttirai peṟutalum iyaṟkaiyeṉṟu koḷva tallatu avaṟṟiṟkuk kāraṇaṅkūṟalākā teṉṟapaṭi. 
20.	meykaḷ eṉak kūṟavēṇṭiyatai meyyeṉa orumaiyākak kūṟiṉa maiyiṉ ataṟkiyaiya araiyeṉa orumaiyāṟ kūṟiṉāreṉpār meyyeṉṉum oṟṟumaipaṟṟi araiyeṉṟār eṉṟār.
21.	cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟeṉpavark kitu kūṟalvēṇṭāvākaliṉ piṟaṉ kōṭ kūṟal eṉṟum utti eṉṟār. 
22.	itaṉai makarakkuṟukkam puḷḷipeṟutalai vitikkavanta cūttirameṉ pārumuḷar.
23.	varivaṭivaik kuṟittu niṉṟatu.
24.	akaram nīṅkiyē pōka eṉṟatu - akaram mīḷavum vantu ēṟātu nīṅkip pōka eṉṟapaṭi. eṉavē vārātoḻiya eṉpatām.
25.	aṟṟu eṉpatu - māṭṭēṟu.
26.	puḷḷipeṟutal eṉṟatu - kā ṅā eṉpaṉa akkālattu ka. ṅa. eṉap puḷḷi peṟṟu vaḻaṅkiṉamaiyai. uyir taṉmaiyum, taṉmaiyumuṭaiya teṉpatu peṟṟām eṉa muṭikka. pulappaṭātu niṟṟal iyalpākiya akaramum, pulappaṭṭu niṟṟal ēṟiya uyirum eṉka. uyir niṟkuntaṉmai yum, uyir varuntaṉmaiyumuṭaiyatu eṉak kūṭṭikkoḷka.  
27.	vaḻi - piṉ.
28.	kuṟi - peyar.
29.	ippeyarāṉē eṉṟatu, vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai eṉṉum peyar kaḷai. uraikārar tām eḻuttu aruvaṉṟu uruveṉpatai itaṉālum valiyuṟuttamāṟu uruvāyiṉa eṉa īṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāreṉka.
30.	ic cūttirattukku uraiyāciriyar mēṟcolliya patiṉeṭṭu meyyum  tammai moḻippaṭutti vaḻaṅkutaluḷatāmiṭattu meymmayakka meṉṟum uṭaṉilai mayakkameṉṟum iruvakaiya; avai mayaṅku muṟai yārāyuṅ kālattu eṉṟu kūṟi meymmayakka meṉpataṟku vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkameṉṟum, uṭaṉilai mayakka meṉpataṟkut taṉṉoṭutāṉ mayaṅkutaleṉṟum poruḷ koṇṭaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar `meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉilai' eṉpatai meymmayaṅku nilai uṭaṉmayaṅku nilai eṉakkoṇṭu, uṭaṉmayaṅku nilai eṉpataṟku meykaḷ uyiruṭaṉ kūṭiniṉṟu uyirmeyyōṭum taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkameṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉar.
	uṭaṉilai meymmayakkattiṟku avar kūṟiya poruḷiṉpaṭi uyirmey uyirmeyyōṭu mayaṅkumiṭattuk karu eṉa mayaṅkum. āṇṭu kakarattiluḷḷa akaramum ru eṉṉum eḻuttiluḷḷa rakaramum mayaṅkiyataṉṟik kakara akaramum rakara ukaramum mayaṅkiyatiṉṟām. ēṉeṉiṉ `meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu nilaiyē' eṉpatu viti yākaliṉ. ātaliṉ, uraiyāciriyar karuttē poruttamā meṉka. naṉṉūlār karuttu mituvēyām. 
31.	īṟākak kūṟukiṉṟateṉ ṟuṇarka eṉa muṭikka.  
32.	`maruviṉpāttiya' eṉpatu 76-m cūttirattiṉ ōraṭi. accūttirattu viṉait tokaiyaiyum paṇputtokaiyaiyum pirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭā teṉṟu kūṟuvārākaliṉ oru moḻiyākak koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu karuttu.
33.	eṇku - karaṭi. ñāṇ - kayiṟu. eṇvaṭṭu eṉpataṟku - eṇṇappaṭum vaṭṭu eṉap poruḷ koḷka.
34.	irumoḻippuṇarcci kāṭṭiṉ kūṟiyatu kūṟaleṉṉum kuṟṟamāmeṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal poruntātu. ēṉeṉil mayakkam vēṟu puṇarcci vēṟākaliṉ. vēṟāmāṟṟai, mayakkamuḷḷaṉavum iyalpātalaṉṟit tirintum mayakkamillātaṉa mayakka muḷḷaṉavākat tirintum puṇartalāṉaṟika. 
	poṉkuṭam - poṟkuṭam. itu mayakkamuḷḷaṉa tirintaṉa. kaltītu - kaṟṟītu itu mayakkamillātaṉa tirintu puṇarntaṉa.
35.	ōyvu, ōrvu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ vakara ukaramum, ovvōr eṉpatil vakara ōkāramum, āyñar eṉpatil ñakara akaramum moḻikku mutalākā eḻuttukkaḷ. avai ya ra ḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟōṭum mayaṅki vantamaikku īṇṭu utāraṇamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
36.	orutalaimoḻitaleṉṉu muttiyāvatu ōratikārattiṟ collaṟ pālataṉai vēṟōratikārattiṟ colli avvilakkaṇamē āṇṭum peṟavaittal. collatikārattiṟ collaṟ pālatāya cuṭṭai eḻuttiṟ kūṟi ataṉaiyē colliṟkum peṟavaittal.
37.	uṇkā - uṇpēṉā?
38.	ākāramum ēkāramum eṉavum pāṭam. makāliṅkaiyar patippu nōkkuka.
39.	moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉaiyu muṭ ṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉṉu muttiyāvatu eṭuttōtiya poruṇmaik kēṟṟa vakaiyāṉap poruṇmaikkuc collātatoṉṟu koḷḷa vaittal. iṅkē eṭuttōtiya poruḷ viṉā ā ē ō ammūṉṟum eṉpatu. avaṟṟō ṭeṭuttōtāta poruḷ yā eṉpatu. ataṉaiyum viṉāveṉṟu ivvuttiyāṟ koḷka eṉṟapaṭi. iṉameṉṟatu uyir meyyātaliṟ pōlum. 
40.	iṟantu - kaṭantu.
41.	itu - ic cūttiram.
42.	toḻiṟpeyar - viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyar. 


moḻimarapu
moḻikaḷukku eḻuttāṉvarum marapuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ moḻimarapeṉappaṭṭatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum eḻuttāṉām moḻiyatu marapuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻimarapeṉak kāraṇappeyarttāyiṟṟu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivviyaliṉ peyarkkāraṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. ivviyaluṭ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam taṉiniṉṟa eḻuttiṟkaṉṟi moḻiyiṭai yeḻuttiṟku eṉa iḷampūraṇar kūṟuvar. eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷai moḻippaṭut ticaikkuṅkāl moḻiyil niṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷukku uḷavām iyalpiṉai yuṇarttuvatu ivviyaleṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ivviyaliṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ 49.
moḻiyiṉaic cārntu olittalaiyē iyalpākavuṭaiya cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiṟkum avaitām kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram āytam eṉa muṉṉaruraitta muṟaiyē ivviyalil ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār. ammūṉṟum oru moḻi puṇarmoḻi ākiya īriṭattum varumeṉakkoṇṭu avaṟṟai orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram, oru moḻiyāytam, puṇar moḻiyāytam eṉa āṟākappirittu muṟaiyē 1-mutal 6-varai yuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil uṇartti 7-ām cūttirattil avvoru moḻi yāytattiṟku ōr ilakkaṇamuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivai moḻiyiṭaic cārttiyuṇarappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum oruvāṟṟāṉ eḻutteṉak koḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ākalāṉum nūṉ marapaiyaṭuttu moḻimarapiṉ mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟaiyaṭuttu uyiraḷapeṭai, moḻi yākkam, meykaḷiṉ iyakkam, īroṟ ṟuṭaṉilai, makarak kuṟukkam, eḻuttuppōli, aikārak kuṟukkam, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ, moḻikkīṟā meḻuttukkaḷ, eṉpaṉa uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. ivaiyellām taṉi yeḻuttukkaḷilaṉṟi moḻikaḷil vaittu aṟintukoḷḷutaṟ kuriyaṉavātaliṉ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
- ka. veḷḷaivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvaricai - 10, pak. 167-168

iraṇṭāvatu
moḻimarapu
34.	kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum
				yāveṉ ciṉaimicai yuraiyacaik kiḷavik
				kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē
eṉpatu cūttiram. mēl eḻuttu uṇarttip piṉṉar, avai tammuṭ toṭaru māṟum uṇartti, avveḻuttāṉām moḻiyatu marapu uṇarttu kiṉṟamaiyiṉ, ivvōttu moḻimarapu eṉak kāraṇap peyarttāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram - muṉṉarc cārntu varumeṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuṭ orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ  kiḷavikku varūum - tāṉ kūṟum poruḷaik kōṭaṟku oruvaṉai etirmukamākkuñ colliṟkup poruntavarum, āvayiṉ - am miyā eṉṉum iṭaiccollaic collumiṭattu, yāveṉ ciṉaimicai makaram ūrntu - yā veṉṉum uṟuppiṉ mēlatāy mutalāy niṉṟa makaravoṟṟiṉai yēṟi, kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum - kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kēṇmiyā, ceṉmiyā eṉa varum. kēḷeṉṟatu uraiyacaik kiḷavi. ataṉaic cārntu taṉakku  iyalpiṉṟi niṉṟatu miyāveṉṉum iṭaic col. avviṭaic col mutalum, ataṉiṟ piriyum yā ataṟku uṟuppumā meṉṟu karuti yāveṉ ciṉai eṉṟār. miyā iṭam; makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yāvum, ikaram arai māttiraiyātaṟkuc cārpu. ivviṭaic col taṉittu niṟṟal āṟṟāmaiyiṟ kēḷeṉpataṉōṭu cārntu oru collāyē niṉṟuḻi iṭainiṉṟa ikaram orumoḻi yiṭattuk kuṟṟiyalikaramāy varutalāṉum āṇṭu uṇarttaṟkuc ciṟappiṉmaiyāṉum īṇṭup pōttantu kūṟiṉār. ūrnteṉavē kuṟṟiyalikaramum uyireṉpatu peṟṟām, uyirkkallatu ēṟutaliṉmaiyiṉ.	(1)
35.	puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalumu rittē
				yuṇarak kūṟiṉ 2muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
itu, kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumeṉ kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalum urittē - ak kuṟṟiyalikaram oru moḻikkaṇṇaṉṟi iru moḻi tammiṟ puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilaimaikkaṇṇuṅ kuṟukutalurittu; uṇarakkūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum - ataṟku iṭamum paṟṟuk kōṭum uṇarak kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉ avai kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariya luḷḷē kūṟappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṟukalumeṉṉumiṭattu ummaiyai nilaiyiṭaiyumeṉa māṟik kūṭṭuka. yakaram varuvaḻi (eḻut. 410) eṉṉuñ cūt tirattu yakaram - iṭam; ukarañ cārnta valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
utāraṇam: nākiyātu, varakiyātu, teḷkiyātu, eḵkiyātu, kokki yātu, kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum. itu moḻivām eṉṉum utti.	(2)
36.	neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
				kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
iḵtu, orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntē - kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttukkaḷ āṟiṉaiyum ūrntu, neṭṭeḻuttimparun toṭar moḻi īṟṟum - neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum aivakait toṭarmoḻiyiṉ iṟutiyiṉum niṟṟal vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
3neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉ, toṭarmoḻiyiṉatu īṟeṉa nilatta takalampōla oṉṟiyaṟkiḻamaippaṭṭu niṉṟaṉa, am moḻiyiṟ ṟīrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram nillāmaiyiṉ. vallāṟu paṇputtokai. muṟṟummai tokkuniṉṟatu. atikāra muṟaimaiyeṉṉum uttiyāṉ niṟṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu varuvikka.
utāraṇam:	nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum. ivvaṟuvakaiyum iṭam; valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu. eṉavē, moḻikku īṟātalum peṟṟām. perumuracu tirumuracu eṉpaṉa iru moḻikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. paracu, 4iṅku, ētu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaravīṟākiya vaṭamoḻic citaivu. tarukku, aṇukku eṉpaṉa viṉaikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟimuṭiya arai māttirai yāy niṟṟalum, muṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟi muṭiyāmaiyun tammuḷ vēṟṟumai.	(3)
37.		iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
					kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻikkaṇ taṉ arai māttiraiyiṟ kuṟuki varum eṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟukum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē - av vukaram orumoḻiyuḷaṉṟip puṇarmoḻi yiṭaippaṭiṉ taṉ arai māttiraiyiṉuṅ kuṟukum iṭaṉum uṇṭu; kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉa - ataṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭup peṟuva teṉiṉ, ataṉ puṇarcci muṟaimai aṟiyuṅ kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi (eḻut. 409) eṉpataṉuḷ val leḻuttut toṭarmoḻiyum valleḻuttu varum vaḻiyum iṭam; īṟṟu valleḻuttup paṟṟukkōṭu.
utāraṇam: cekkukkaṇai, cukkukkoṭu eṉa varum. ivai araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟukiyavāṟu ēṉaiyavaṟṟoṭu paṭuttu uṇarka. iṭaṉumeṉavē itu ciṟupāṉmai yāyiṟṟu. 	(4)
38.		kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
					yuyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē.
itu, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āytam orumoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:āytap puḷḷi - āytamākiya oṟṟu, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittu - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyiroṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttāṟiṉ mēliṭatta tāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.
5vallāṟaṉ micait teṉṟataṉāṉum, īṇṭup puḷḷi yeṉṟataṉāṉum, āytat toṭarmoḻi (eḻut. 409) eṉa mēṟ kūṟutalāṉum, uyireṉṟatu īṇṭup perumpāṉmaiyuṅ kuṟṟukaramēyām; ciṟupāṉmai ēṉai uyirkaḷaiyuṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam:	eḵku, kaḵcu, 6kaḵṭu, kaḵtu, kaḵpu, kaḵṟu, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉa varum. kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉpaṉavaṟṟai meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci yeṉṟataṉālum, īṇṭup puḷḷi eṉṟataṉāṉum āytamum meyyāyiṟṟu. aḵkāmai veḵkāmai aḵki veḵki aḵkam eṉap piṟavuyirkaḷōṭum vantatu. kaḵciyā teṉat tirintatuvuṅ kuṟṟiyalu karattōṭu puṇarntatām.	(5)
39.		īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉu micaimai tōṉṟum.
iḵtu - avvāytam puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉum - nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkum iṭattum, icaimai tōṉṟum - taṉ araimāttiraiyē icaikkun taṉmai tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu eṉavarum. ivvīṟu iyalumāṟu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluṭ peṟutum.  īṇṭum iṭam, kuṟṟeḻuttumēl varum valleḻuttu.	(6)
40.		uruviṉu micaiyiṉu marukit tōṉṟu
					moḻikkuṟip pellā meḻutti ṉiyalā
					vāyta maḵkāk kālai yāṉa.
iḵtu etiratupōṟṟal eṉṉum uttiyāṟ ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki āytattiṟku eytiyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: 7uruviṉum icaiyiṉum arukit tōṉṟuṅ kuṟippu moḻiyum - niṟattiṉkaṇṇum ōcaiyiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai āytan tōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟit talaiyuṭaiya collum, ellāmoḻiyum - avaiyoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum, eḻuttiṉiyalā - oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ pōla araimāttiraiyiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai mikku naṭantu, āytam aḵkāk kālaiyāṉa - āytañ curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, īṇṭu ārāycciyiṉṟēṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟum, oṟṟaḷa peṭuppiṉu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa (cey. 18) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuk kaṇṇ ṭaṇṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum  eṉpuḻik kaṇṇṇeṉpatu cīrnilai eytiṉāṟ pōlak kaḵḵ ṟeṉṉuṅ kallata rattam eṉa niṟattiṉkaṇṇum, cuḵḵ ṟeṉṉun taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇai eṉa icaiyiṉ kaṇṇum vanta āytam oru māttirai peṟṟuc cīrnilai yeytuṅkāl, āṇṭup peṟukiṉṟa oru māttiraikku īṇṭu etiratu pōṟṟi viti kūṟiṉār, āytam atikārappaṭṭamai kaṇṭu. eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kaiyarā yiṉṉuyir, veḵḵku vārkkillai vīṭu eṉṟu ēṉaiyiṭattum vantaṉa. oṟṟaḷa peṭukkumāṟu ivvatikārattuk kūṟiṟṟilar, aḵtu uyiraḷa peṭaipōlac cīrnilai yeytutalum acainilaiyān taṉmaiyumuṭaiyatāyc ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ. itaṉāṉē oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyum oru māttirai peṟumeṉpatu peṟṟām.
eḻuttiṉeṉṟa iṉ uvamapporuḷ. iyalāveṉṟatu ceyyā veṉṉum viṉaiyeccam.
ivvāṟaṉṟi ikkuṟippuccoṟkaḷ āytam iraṇṭiṭṭu eḻutappaṭāveṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṟ ceyyuḷiyaloṭu māṟupaṭṭu māṟu koḷak kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.		(7)
41.		kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikku
					neṭṭeḻut timpa rottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
iḵtu - etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṉum utti paṟṟic ceyyuḷiyalai nōkki nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ (eḻut. 6) eṉa muṟkūṟiya aḷapeṭaiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu icai niṟaikkum - aḷapeṭuttuk kūṟākkāṟ kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya avvaḷapeṭaic coṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōcaiyai niṟaikkum; avai yāvaiyoṉiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta kuṟṟeḻuttē - neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉākat tamakku iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: āa, īi, ūu, ēe, ōo eṉa varum. kuṉṟicai moḻi eṉṟataṟku icaikuṉṟu moḻi eṉṟumām. iṉamottalāvatu piṟappum puṇarcciyum ōcaiyum vaṭivum ottal. īṇṭu moḻiyeṉṟatu aḷapeṭaiyacai nilai (ceyyuḷiyal 17) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattu eṭṭu iyaṟ cīriṉpāṟ paṭukiṉṟa eṇvakai aḷapeṭaic coṟkaḷaiyum. avai āa, kaṭāa, āaḻi, paṭāakai, āaṅku, āavatu, pukāarttu, virāayatu eṉpaṉavām. 8kaṭṭaḷai koḷḷā āciriyar ivaṟṟait taṉinilai mutaṉilai iṭainilai iṟuti nilai yeṉṟum aṭakkupa. iṉi moḻi yeṉṟataṟkut taṉi nilai ēḻaṉaiyumē koḷḷiṉ, oḻinta iyaṟcīrppāṟ paṭum aḷapeṭai kōṭaṟku iṭamiṉmai uṇarka.	(8)
42.			aiau eṉṉu māyī reḻuttiṟ
					kikara vukara micainiṟai vākum.
iḵtu  otta kuṟṟeḻuttu illātaṉa aḷapeṭukkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
ituvum etiratu pōṟṟal.
itaṉ poruḷ: ai au eṉṉum āyīreḻuttiṟku - tamakku iṉamillāta aikāra aukārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum avviraṇṭeḻuttiṟkum ikara ukaram icainiṟaivākum - īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷaic cārttikkūṟa, avai akkuṉṟicai moḻikkaṇ niṉṟu ōcaiyai niṟaippaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.
aii, auu eṉa niraṉiṟaiyākak koḷka. ivaṟṟai muṟkūṟiya iyaṟcīreṭṭiṟkum ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭikkoḷka.
ittuṇaiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu.	(9)
43.			neṭṭeḻut tēḻē 
					yōreḻut torumoḻi.
iḵtu ōreḻuttorumoḻi uṇarttutal nutaliya vaṟṟuḷ neṭṭeḻuttāṉām moḻiyākkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē - neṭṭeḻut tākiya uyirkaḷēḻum, ōreḻuttorumoḻi - ōreḻut tāṉākum oru moḻi yām eṉṟavāṟu.
muṟṟummai tokuttu īṟṟacai ēkāram virittār.
utāraṇam: ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, ō eṉa varum. aukāram uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārātu. ū eṉpatu tacai. iḵtu uyirkkum uyir meykkum viti. kā tī pū cē tai kō kau eṉa varum. 9ivai tammaiyuṇaraniṉṟa vaḻi eḻuttām; iṭainiṉṟu poruḷuṇarttiyavaḻic collām. neṭṭeḻut tēṟiya mey neṭṭeḻuttāyuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttēṟiya mey kuṟṟeḻuttāyum niṟṟalēyaṉṟi meykku neṭumaiyum kuṟumaiyum iṉmai uṇarka.		(10)
44.	kuṟṟeḻut taintu moḻiniṟai pilavē.
itu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyākā; avaṟṟuṭ cila moḻi yākumeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum - kuṟṟeḻuttākiya uyiraintum, moḻiniṟaipu ilavē - tāmē niṟaintu niṉṟu moḻiyātal ila; cila meyyoṭukūṭi  niṟaintu niṉṟu moḻiyām eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: tu, no eṉa varum. ivai uyirmeyk kaṇṇallatu vārāmaiyāṉum, uyirkkaṇṇum, ēṉai akaramum ekaramum ak koṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉat taṉittu niṉṟu uṇarttalāṟṟātu iṭaiccollāyp peyaraic cārntu niṉṟu cuṭṭup poruḷum viṉāp poruḷum uṇarttutalāṉum, niṟaipila veṉṟār. 10muṟṟummai īṇṭu eccappaṭṭu niṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka.	(11)
45.	ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
				yiraṇṭiṟan ticaikkun toṭarmoḻi yuḷappaṭa
				mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
itu, muṉṉar meymmayakkam uṭaṉilaimayakkaṅ kūṟa lāṉum, īṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttēḻē (eḻut. 43) eṉpataṉāṉum, eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu kūṟiṉār. ammoḻikku ic cūttirattāṟ peyarum muṟaiyun tokaiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟār.
itaṉ poruḷ: ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttoru moḻi iraṇṭiṟantu icaikkun toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa - ōreḻut tāṉākum; oru moḻiyum iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākum; orumoḻiyum iraṇṭaṉai iṟantu palavāṟṟāṉ icaikkun toṭarmoḻi yuṭaṉē kūṭa, moḻi nilai mūṉṟē - moḻikaḷiṉ nilaimai mūṉṟēyām, tōṉṟiya neṟiyē - avai tōṉṟiya vaḻakkuneṟik kaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	ā kā nā: ōreḻuttorumoḻi. maṇi varaku koṟṟaṉ: īreḻuttorumoḻi. kuravu aravu: mūveḻut toru moḻi. kaṇaviri: nāleḻut toru moḻi. akattiyaṉār: aiyeḻuttoru moḻi. tirucciṟṟampalam: āṟeḻuttoru moḻi. perum paṟṟappuliyūr: ēḻeḻuttorumoḻi.
11ōreḻuttorumoḻiyun toṭarmoḻiyu meṉṉātu īreḻuttorumoḻiyum ōtiṉār, cila pala eṉṉun tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki.
āciriya oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum eḻutteṉṟu koṇṭaṉarā taliṉ, mā kā eṉa niṉṟa coṟkaḷ māl kāl eṉa oṟṟaṭuttuḻi oṟṟiṉāṉ vēṟuporuḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟai īreḻut torumoḻi yeṉṟum, nāku varaku eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṟ kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ colloṭukūṭip poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟai, īreḻuttorumoḻi mūveḻuttoru moḻi yeṉṟuṅ kōṭum eṉpārkku, āciriyar poruḷaik karutātu māttirai kuṟaintamai paṟṟi uyirilleḻuttu meṇṇap paṭāa (ceyyuḷiyal. 44) kuṟilē neṭilē kuṟiliṇai (ceyyuḷiyal. 3) eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttiraṅkaḷāl ivaṟṟai eḻut teṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā eṉṟu vilakkuvarātaliṉ, avaṟṟāl īṇṭu īreḻut torumoḻiyum mūveḻuttoru moḻiyum koḷḷiṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu maṟukka.
